*** Keywords ***
Calculate data of every bettype in match
    [Arguments]    ${ac_Match}    ${ac_Level_login}    ${IsStackMode}=0
    [Documentation]    This keyword to calculate data of every bettype in match by row
    ...
    ...    ***Step***
    ...
    ...    Get data by row and calculate.
    ...
    ...    ***Param***
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Match}: input Match locator
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_login}: input level of login account
    ...
    ...    ***History***
    ...
    ...    2017-04-25: Kingsley created.
    Wait Until Page Not Contains Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    10s    #Wait until BlockUI end
    : FOR    ${ac_iBettype}    IN RANGE    1    5000
    \    ${ac_Bettype_Attribute}    Get Element Attribute    ${ac_Match}/following::tr[${ac_iBettype}]@class
    \    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Bettype_Attribute}'=='sub-total-row' and '${IsStackMode}'=='0'    VerifyTotalMatchWinlossDetails_AllRow_NormalMode    ${ac_Match}/following::tr[@class='sub-total-row']    ${ac_Level_login}
    \    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Bettype_Attribute}'=='sub-total-row' and '${IsStackMode}'=='1'    VerifyTotalMatchWinlossDetails_AllRow_StackMode    ${ac_Match}/following::tr[@class='sub-total-row']    ${ac_Level_login}
    \    Exit For Loop If    '${ac_Bettype_Attribute}'=='sub-total-row'
    \    Run Keyword If    '${IsStackMode}'=='1'    VerifyTotalMatchWinlossDetails_AllRow_StackMode    ${ac_Match}/following::tr[${ac_iBettype}]    ${ac_Level_login}
    \    ...    ELSE    VerifyTotalMatchWinlossDetails_AllRow_NormalMode    ${ac_Match}/following::tr[${ac_iBettype}]    ${ac_Level_login}
    \    ${ac_Bettypename}    Get Text    ${ac_Match}/following::tr[${ac_iBettype}]/td[1]

Compare value with boundary range
    [Arguments]    ${ac_value1}    ${ac_value2}    ${ac_boundary}    ${msg}=${EMPTY}
    [Documentation]    This keyword used to compare 2 value with boundary range
    ...
    ...    ***Param***
    ...
    ...    ${ac_value1}
    ...
    ...    ${ac_value2}
    ...
    ...    ${ac_boundary}: boundary value. Ex: 0.5
    ...
    ...    ***History***
    ...
    ...    2017-04-25: Kingsley created
    ## Get abs of value1 and value2
    ${ac_result}    Evaluate    abs(${ac_value1} - ${ac_value2})
    ## Compare with boundary value
    Run Keyword And Continue On Failure    Should Be True    ${ac_result}<=${ac_boundary}    ${msg} ${ac_value1} != ${ac_value2}

Open ForeCast Popup by JS
    [Arguments]    ${ac_btnInfol}
    ## Set Variable
    ${ac_btnInfohref}=    Get Element Attribute    ${ac_btnInfol}@href
    ${ac_btnInfohref}=    Replace String    ${ac_btnInfohref}    javascript:    ${EMPTY}
    Execute Javascript    ${ac_btnInfohref}

Report_VerifyFunctionExportExcel
    [Arguments]    ${ac_Excel_Element}    ${ac_ReportName}=${EMPTY}    ${ac_filename}=${EMPTY}    ${ac_Sheetname}=Sheet 1    ${ac_Cellname}=A4    ${ac_existData}=True
    [Documentation]    This action is used to verify function export excel file for betlist microsite
    ...
    ...    ${ac_ReportName}: Name of report need to export
    ...
    ...    ${ac_filename}: The file name of report need to export
    Remove Files    ${cfReportDownloadFolder}\\*${ac_filename}*.*
    Comment    *****Capture screenshot before export excel********
    Capture Page Screenshot
    Click Control    ${ac_Excel_Element}
    ## Check web is still ok
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_ReportName}'!='${EMPTY}'    Verify Report General    ${ac_ReportName}    no
    ## Check excel file is generated
    Wait Until Keyword Succeeds    2min    2s    File Should Exist    ${cfReportDownloadFolder}\\${ac_filename}.xls
    Comment    ***Wait down excel file completed***
    Sleep    4s
    Comment    *****VP: Open excel file and check content********
    Open Excel Without Keep Process    ${cfReportDownloadFolder}\\${ac_filename}.xls
    ${ac_expected_text}    Read Cell Data By Name    ${ac_Sheetname}    ${ac_Cellname}
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_existData}'=='True'    Should Not Be Equal    ${ac_expected_text}    No information is available    Excel file should contains data    True
    ...    ELSE    Should Be Equal    ${ac_expected_text}    No information is available    Excel file should not contains data    True

Select Date Range Report WL Analysis Chart
    [Arguments]    ${Locator}    ${Month}    ${Year}
    [Documentation]    This is action is used to select Filter Report by input Date Range and Product List
    ...
    ...    *Note*: *Don't* submit
    ...
    ...    Param:
    ...
    ...    ${fromDate}
    ...
    ...    ${toDate}
    ...
    ...    ${DataRange}: Today/Yesterday/This Week/Last Week/This Month/Last Month/Since Last Month/Specific Dates or ${Empty} in case selected FromDate and ToDate.
    ...
    ...    ${productList}
    ...
    ...    ********History***********
    ...
    ...    2016-12-16: Kingsley
    Close Announcement Popup
    Select Frame    main
    #check DateFilter Report
    ${Previous_year}    Evaluate    ${Year}-1
    ${ac_IsDateRange}    Run Keyword And Return Status    Element Should Be Visible    ${Locator}
    #Select From date Current report
    Run Keyword If    ${ac_IsDateRange}    Click Element    ${Locator}
    Capture Page Screenshot
    ${ac_IsYearExpected}    Run Keyword And Return Status    Wait Until Element Is Visible    ${Locator}//..//div[contains(@class,'monpickr-dropdown ui-monpickr')]//li[@class='month-wrap ui-corner-all selected' and @year='${Year}']    20
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_IsYearExpected}'=='False'    Click Element    ${Locator}//..//li[contains(@class,'year ui-corner-all')]/a[@year='${Previous_year}']
    ${ac_IsMonthExpected}    Run Keyword And Return Status    Wait Until Element Is Visible    ${Locator}//..//div[contains(@class,'monpickr-dropdown ui-monpickr')]//li[@class='month-wrap ui-corner-all selected' and @year='${Year}']//a[@month='${Month}']    20
    Run Keyword If    ${ac_IsMonthExpected}    Click Element    ${Locator}//..//div[contains(@class,'monpickr-dropdown ui-monpickr')]//li[@class='month-wrap ui-corner-all selected' and @year='${Year}']//a[@month='${Month}']
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_IsMonthExpected}'=='False'    Log    The Month-Year is not visible

Select Report Filter
    [Arguments]    ${action}    ${fromDate}=${EMPTY}    ${toDate}=${EMPTY}    ${toDay}=${EMPTY}    ${yesterday}=${EMPTY}    ${productList}=${EMPTY}
    [Documentation]    This is action is used to select Filter Report
    Run Keyword If    '${fromDate}'!='${EMPTY}'    Select From Date    ${fromDate}
    Run Keyword If    '${toDate}'!='${EMPTY}'    Select To Date    ${toDate}
    Run Keyword If    '${today}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Element At Coordinates    ${in_m_btnToday}
    Run Keyword If    '${yesterday}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Element At Coordinates    ${in_m_btnYesterday}
    Run Keyword If    '${productList}'=='All'    Set Checkbox    ${in_m_rptOld_chkAll}    check
    Run Keyword If    '${action}'=='submit'    click element    ${in_m_btnSubmit}

Smartphone Check show/hide filter report tooltips translation
    [Arguments]    ${ac_icon_arrow_up_locator}    ${ac_icon_arrow_down_locator}
    [Documentation]    This action to check the show/hide mode report tooltips translation
    ...
    ...    ${ac_icon_arrow_up_locator}: control locator of icon arrow up
    ...
    ...    ${ac_icon_arrow_down_locator}: control locator of icon arrow down
    ...
    ...    *****History*******
    ...
    ...    20170110: Kingsley created
    ...
    ...    ********List of TCs are automated*********:
    ...
    ...    Alpha-6185:Check tooltips translation_English
    ...
    ...    Alpha-6186:Check tooltips translation_Chinese Traditional
    ...
    ...    Alpha-6187:Check tooltips translation_Chinese Simplified
    ...
    ...    Alpha-6188:Check tooltips translation_Thai
    ...
    ...    Alpha-6215:Check tooltips translation_Vietnamese
    #Set tooltips text
    ${CN_Traditional_hide}    Set Variable    隱藏篩選
    ${CN_Simplified_hide}    Set Variable    隐藏筛选
    ${Thai_hide}    Set Variable    ซ่อนตัวกรอง
    ${Vietnamese_hide}    Set Variable    Ẩn bộ lọc dữ liệu
    ${English_hide}    Set Variable    Hide filters
    #--------------
    ${CN_Traditional_show}    Set Variable    顯示篩選
    ${CN_Simplified_show}    Set Variable    显示筛选
    ${Thai_show}    Set Variable    แสดงผลตัวกรอง
    ${Vietnamese_show}    Set Variable    Hiện bộ lọc dữ liệu
    ${English_show}    Set Variable    Show filters
    Run Keyword And Continue On Failure    Verify report stack/normal and show/hide language translation    繁體中文    ${CN_Traditional_hide}    ${CN_Traditional_show}    ${ac_icon_arrow_up_locator}    ${ac_icon_arrow_down_locator}
    Run Keyword And Continue On Failure    Verify report stack/normal and show/hide language translation    简体中文    ${CN_Simplified_hide}    ${CN_Simplified_show}    ${ac_icon_arrow_up_locator}    ${ac_icon_arrow_down_locator}
    Run Keyword And Continue On Failure    Verify report stack/normal and show/hide language translation    ภาษาไทย    ${Thai_hide}    ${Thai_show}    ${ac_icon_arrow_up_locator}    ${ac_icon_arrow_down_locator}
    Run Keyword And Continue On Failure    Verify report stack/normal and show/hide language translation    Tiếng Việt    ${Vietnamese_hide}    ${Vietnamese_show}    ${ac_icon_arrow_up_locator}    ${ac_icon_arrow_down_locator}
    Run Keyword And Continue On Failure    Verify report stack/normal and show/hide language translation    English    ${English_hide}    ${English_show}    ${ac_icon_arrow_up_locator}    ${ac_icon_arrow_down_locator}

    ...

    ...
    ...    *Step*

    ...    - 2 VP: check Table of stack mode is visible and some colums are correct

    Wait Until Page Does Not Contain Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    10s

    Wait Until Element Is Enabled    ${in_m_report_icon_stack_mode}    10s
    Click Element    ${in_m_report_icon_stack_mode}
    Comment    *****2 VP: check Table of stack mode is visible and some colums are correct**********
    Wait Until Element Is Visible    ${in_m_report_Table_stack_mode}    10s    Stack mode is not showed
    Wait Until Element Is Visible    ${in_m_report_Table_stack_mode}//th[contains(@class,'visible-stack-mode')]    10s    Stack mode is not showed
    ${ac_SuperHeaderColumn}    Set Variable If    '${ac_IsSuperColumn}'=='Yes'    Super\n(WL/Comm/Total)    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_MasterHeaderColumn}    Set Variable If    '${ac_IsMasterColumn}'=='Yes'    Master\n(WL/Comm/Total)    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_column_stackmode_list_expected}    Create List    Bet Type    Turnover\nGross Comm    Member\n(WL/Comm/Total)    Agent\n(WL/Comm/Total)    ${ac_SuperHeaderColumn}
    ...    ${ac_MasterHeaderColumn}    Company\n(Comm/Total)
    ${ac_column_stackmode_list_actual}    Create List
    ${ac_column_BetType}    Get Text    ${in_m_report_table_header_Smartphone}//th[contains(@class,'visible-stack-mode') and contains(text(),'Bet Type')]
    ${ac_column_TO_GrossComm}    Get Text    ${in_m_report_table_header_Smartphone}//th[contains(@class,'visible-stack-mode') and contains(text(),'Turnover')]
    ${ac_column_Member}    Get Text    ${in_m_report_table_header_Smartphone}//th[contains(@class,'visible-stack-mode') and contains(text(),'Member')]
    ${ac_column_Agent}    Get Text    ${in_m_report_table_header_Smartphone}//th[contains(@class,'visible-stack-mode') and contains(text(),'Agent')]
    ${ac_column_Master}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_IsMasterColumn}'=='Yes'    Get Text    ${in_m_report_table_header_Smartphone}//th[contains(@class,'visible-stack-mode') and contains(text(),'Master')]
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_column_Super}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_IsSuperColumn}'=='Yes'    Get Text    ${in_m_report_table_header_Smartphone}//th[contains(@class,'visible-stack-mode') and contains(text(),'Super')]
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_column_Company}    Get Text    ${in_m_report_table_header_Smartphone}//th[contains(@class,'visible-stack-mode') and contains(text(),'Company')]
    Append To List    ${ac_column_stackmode_list_actual}    ${ac_column_BetType}    ${ac_column_TO_GrossComm}    ${ac_column_Member}    ${ac_column_Agent}    ${ac_column_Super}
    ...    ${ac_column_Master}    ${ac_column_Company}
    Lists Should Be Equal    ${ac_column_stackmode_list_expected}    ${ac_column_stackmode_list_actual}    Content of columns in stack mode are not correct.
    Comment    ******3 VP: check icon normal mode is present then click it********
    Wait Until Element Is Enabled    ${in_m_report_icon_normal_mode}    10s
    Click Element    ${in_m_report_icon_normal_mode}
    Comment    ******4 VP: check Table stack mode is disable and normal mode is visible*********
    Wait Until Element Is Not Visible    ${in_m_report_Table_stack_mode}    10s    Stack mode still shows
    Wait Until Element Is Not Visible    ${in_m_report_Table_stack_mode}//th[contains(@class,'visible-stack-mode')]    10s    Stack mode still shows

    ${ac_ProductList}    Replace String    ${ac_ProductList}    \n    ,    -1
    ${ac_ProductOffered}    Create List
    Comment    ***Check Casino***
    ${ac_IsCasino}    Run Keyword And Return Status    Should Contain    ${ac_ProductList}    ,Casino
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_IsCasino}'=='True'    Append To List    ${ac_ProductOffered}    Casino
    Comment    ***Check Allbet***
    ${ac_IsAllbet}    Run Keyword And Return Status    Should Contain    ${ac_ProductList}    Allbet

    ${ac_IsMacauGames}    Run Keyword And Return Status    Should Contain    ${ac_ProductList}    Macau Games

    Comment    ***Check Fishing World***

    ${ac_ProductOffered_String}    Evaluate    ', '.join(${ac_ProductOffered})
    Comment    ***VP: Check one of Casino, Macau Games, Allbet, Fishing World are offered or not***
    ${ac_IsInfoIcon}    Set Variable If    '${ac_IsCasino}'=='True' or '${ac_IsMacauGames}'=='True' or '${ac_IsAllbet}'=='True' or '${ac_IsFishingWorld}'=='True'    True    False
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_IsCasino}'=='True' or '${ac_IsMacauGames}'=='True' or '${ac_IsAllbet}'=='True' or '${ac_IsFishingWorld}'=='True'    Return From Keyword    ${ac_ProductOffered_String}
    ...    ELSE    Return From Keyword    False
    Comment    Comment    ***VP:( i) Icon must be hide if one of Casino, Macau Games, Allbet, Fishing World are not offered***
    Comment    Run Keyword If    '${ac_IsInfoIcon}'=='False'    Element Should Not Be Visible    //span[@id='icon-information']

    Comment    Run Keyword If    '${ac_IsInfoIcon}'=='True'    Should Contain    ${ac_Tooltip}    ${ac_Info}
    Unselect Frame
    [Return]    ${ac_ListOfferedProduct}

    #get Super account Name using for excel file name
    ${ac_super_upper}    Evaluate    "${super}".upper()
    Comment    ***VP: Check Breadcrumb Super***

    ...    A3    ${ac_existData}
    #get Master account Name using for excel file name
    ${ac_master_upper}    Evaluate    "${master}".upper()
    Comment    ***Drill Down to master***
    ${ac_lnkDownline}    Set Variable    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tbody//a[text()='${master}' or text()='${ac_master_upper}']
    Run Keyword If    '${super}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Element    ${ac_lnkDownline}
    Run Keyword If    '${super}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Report General    COMMISSION BY BET TYPE    yes
    Comment    ***VP: Check Breadcrumb Master***
    Run Keyword If    '${super}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Breadcrumb CommissionByBetType    MASTER COMMISSION BY BET TYPE    ${super}    ${master}    ${agent}
    Run Keyword If    '${super}'=='${EMPTY}' and '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Breadcrumb CommissionByBetType    MASTER COMMISSION BY BET TYPE    ${EMPTY}    ${master}    ${agent}
    Comment    ***Export Excel Master***
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Report_VerifyFunctionExportExcel    ${in_Reports_btnExportToExcel_smartphone}    COMMISSION BY BET TYPE    Commission By Bet Type-${ac_master_upper}-${ac_fromDate_filename}-${ac_toDate_filename}    Sheet 1
    ...    A3    ${ac_existData}

    Smartphone Verify Breadcrumb CommissionByBetType    AGENT COMMISSION BY BET TYPE    ${EMPTY}    ${EMPTY}    ${agent}

    ...    - 2.2 Verify Report Filter Product List
    ...    - 2.3 Check sort function in report outstanding
    ...    - 2.4 Check Breadcrumb Super
    ...    - 2.5 DrillDown Master (If Login level Super)
    ...    - 2.6 Verify report header
    ...    - 2.7 Check sort function in report outstanding
    ...    - 2.8 Check Breadcrumb Master
    ...    - 2.9 DrillDown Agent (If Login level Super or Master)
    ...    - 2.10 Verify report header
    ...    - 2.11 Check sort function in report outstanding
    ...    - 2.12 Check Breadcrumb Agent
    ...    - 2.13 DrillDown Member
    ...    - 2.14 Check Breadcrumb Member (Expected don't have Breadcrumb)
    ...    - 2.15 Click on breadcrumb link of master to drill up to master
    ...    - 2.16 Verify report header
    ...    - 2.17 Click on breadcrumb link of sortuper to drill up to super
    ...    - 2.18 Verify report header
    ...
    ...    *****History*******

    ...    2017-02-27: Kingsley created

    ...    Alpha-7136:[Submit] Button
    Unselect Frame
    select frame    main
    ${agent_Account}    Set Variable    ${ds_View01_A}
    Comment    *****Close Announcement Popup*****
    Close Announcement Popup
    select frame    main
    Comment    ************1 VP: Verify report header************
    Smartphone Verify Report General    ${downLineLevel} OUTSTANDING    no
    Comment    ************2 VP: Verify Report Filter Product List************
    Verify Report Filter Product List    ${cfProductFilterList}
    Comment    *****Click Submit*****
    Click Element    ${in_m_btnSubmit}
    Comment    ************3 VP: Check sort function in report outstanding************
    ${ac_NumberOfRow_master}    Get Matching Xpath Count    ${in_m_report_tablebody_Smartphone}/tr
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}' and ${ac_NumberOfRow_master}>1    Smartphone Check sort function in report outstanding    Master    No    No    No
    Comment    *****Get Super account Name using for excel file name*****
    ${ac_super_name}    Get Substring    ${agent_Account}    \    -4
    ${ac_super_name_upper}    Evaluate    "${ac_super_name}".upper()
    Comment    ************4 VP:Check Breadcrumb Super************
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Breadcrumb OutStanding    MASTER OUTSTANDING    ${ac_super_name}    ${master}    ${agent}
    Comment    *****Close Announcement Popup*****
    Close Announcement Popup
    select frame    main
    Comment    *****5 DrillDown Master (If Login level Super)*****
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Control    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tbody//a[@data-custname='${master}']
    Comment    ************6 VP: Verify report header************
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Report General    AGENT OUTSTANDING    no
    Comment    ************7 VP: Check sort function in report outstanding************
    ${ac_NumberOfRow_Agent}    Get Matching Xpath Count    ${in_m_report_tablebody_Smartphone}/tr
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}' and ${ac_NumberOfRow_Agent}>1    Smartphone Check sort function in report outstanding    Agent    No    No    No
    Comment    *****Get Master account Name using for excel file name*****
    ${ac_master_name}    Get Substring    ${agent_Account}    \    -2
    ${ac_master_name_upper}    Evaluate    "${ac_master_name}".upper()
    Comment    ************8 VP:Check Breadcrumb Master************
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Breadcrumb OutStanding    AGENT OUTSTANDING    ${ac_super_name}    ${ac_master_name}    ${agent}
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'=='${EMPTY}' and '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Breadcrumb OutStanding    AGENT OUTSTANDING    ${EMPTY}    ${ac_master_name}    ${agent}
    Comment    *****9 DrillDown Agent (If Login level Super or Master)*****
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Control    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tbody//a[@data-custname='${agent}']
    Comment    ************10 VP: Verify report header************
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Report General    MEMBER OUTSTANDING    no
    Comment    ************11 VP: Check sort function in report outstanding************
    ${ac_NumberOfRow_Member}    Get Matching Xpath Count    ${in_m_report_tablebody_Smartphone}/tr
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}' and ${ac_NumberOfRow_Member}>1    Smartphone Check sort function in report outstanding    Member    Yes    Yes    Yes
    ...    ELSE IF    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}' and ${ac_NumberOfRow_Member}>1    Smartphone Check sort function in report outstanding    Member    No    Yes
    ...    Yes
    ...    ELSE IF    ${ac_NumberOfRow_Member}>1    Smartphone Check sort function in report outstanding    Member    No    No
    ...    Yes
    Comment    ************12 VP:Check Breadcrumb Agent************
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Breadcrumb OutStanding    MEMBER OUTSTANDING    ${ac_super_name}    ${ac_master_name}    ${agent_Account}
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'=='${EMPTY}' and '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Breadcrumb OutStanding    MEMBER OUTSTANDING    ${EMPTY}    ${ac_master_name}    ${agent_Account}
    Smartphone Verify Breadcrumb OutStanding    MEMBER OUTSTANDING    ${EMPTY}    ${EMPTY}    ${agent_Account}
    Comment    *****13 DrillDown Member*****
    Run Keyword If    '${member}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Control    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tbody//a[@data-custname='${member}']
    Run Keyword If    '${member}'!='${EMPTY}'    Verify Report General    BET LIST (RUNNING)    no
    Comment    ************14 VP:Check Breadcrumb Member************
    Smartphone Verify Breadcrumb OutStanding    Bet List (Running)    ${EMPTY}    ${EMPTY}    ${EMPTY}
    Go back
    Comment    *****15 Click on breadcrumb link of master to drill up to master*****
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Element By Xpath js    //a[@class='breadcrumb-link' and (contains(text(),'${ac_master_name}') or contains(text(),'${ac_master_name_upper}'))]
    sleep    2s
    Comment    ************16 VP: Verify report header************
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Report General    AGENT OUTSTANDING    no
    Comment    *****17 Click on breadcrumb link of Super to drill up to Super*****
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Element By Xpath js    //a[@class='breadcrumb-link' and (contains(text(),'${ac_super_name}') or contains(text(),'${ac_super_name_upper}'))]
    sleep    2s
    Comment    ************18 VP: Verify report header************
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Report General    ${downLineLevel} OUTSTANDING    no
    Unselect Frame

Smartphone Verify Report_OutStanding_Check Navigation
    [Arguments]    ${downLineLevel}=${EMPTY}    ${super}=${EMPTY}    ${master}=${EMPTY}    ${agent}=${EMPTY}    ${member}=${EMPTY}
    [Documentation]    This action is used to check *Navigation* to report Outstanding from DashBoard and Account Info
    ...
    ...    ${downLineLevel}: downline level of account log in
    ...
    ...    ${super}: super username
    ...
    ...    ${master}: master username
    ...
    ...    ${agent}: agent username
    ...
    ...    ${member}: member username
    ...
    ...    *****History*******
    ...
    ...    2017-03-01: Kingsley created
    ...
    ...    ********List of TCs are automated*********:
    ...
    ...    Alpha-7168:Check navigation (Super Level)
    ...
    ...    Alpha-7173:Check navigation (Master Level)
    ...
    ...    Alpha-7176:Check navigation (Agent Level)
    Unselect Frame
    select frame    main
    Smartphone Verify Report General    ${downLineLevel} OUTSTANDING    no
    #Check data default shows all product
    Verify Report Filter Product List    ${cfProductFilterList}
    ${ac_level_login}=    Set Variable if    '${downLineLevel}'=='Master'    Super    '${downLineLevel}'=='Agent'    Master    '${downLineLevel}'=='Member'
    ...    Agent
    ${ac_report_body}    Set Variable    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tbody
    ${ac_report_footer}    Set Variable    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tfoot
    ##Start Navigation report Outstanding from Dashboard
    #Go to Dashboard
    Unselect Frame
    Click Element    ${in_tm_iconHome}
    select frame    main
    #Get SMA and Total Outstanding value on Dashboard
    ${ac_Dashboard_SMA_Outstanding_value}    Get Text    //div[@id='stats']//div[contains(text(),'${ac_level_login}')]/following-sibling::div[*]/a[@class='btn-link']
    ${ac_Dashboard_Total_Outstanding_value}    Get Text    //div[@id='stats']//div[contains(text(),'Total Outstanding')]/following-sibling::div[*]/a[@class='btn-link']
    #click on outstanding value of SMA
    Click Element    //div[@id='stats']//div[contains(text(),'${ac_level_login}')]/following-sibling::div[*]/a[@class='btn-link']
    #Compare value between DashBoard and report Outstanding
    ${ac_SMA_Outstanding_Total_value}    Get Text    ${ac_report_footer}/tr/td[3]
    ${ac_Member_Outstanding_Total_value}    Get Text    ${ac_report_footer}/tr/td[2]
    Should Be Equal    ${ac_Dashboard_SMA_Outstanding_value}    ${ac_SMA_Outstanding_Total_value}
    Should Be Equal    ${ac_Dashboard_Total_Outstanding_value}    ${ac_Member_Outstanding_Total_value}
    #Back to Dashboard
    Unselect Frame
    Click Element    ${in_tm_iconHome}
    select frame    main
    #Get SMA and Total Outstanding value on Dashboard
    ${ac_Dashboard_SMA_Outstanding_value}    Get Text    //div[@id='stats']//div[contains(text(),'${ac_level_login}')]/following-sibling::div[*]/a[@class='btn-link']
    ${ac_Dashboard_Total_Outstanding_value}    Get Text    //div[@id='stats']//div[contains(text(),'Total Outstanding')]/following-sibling::div[*]/a[@class='btn-link']
    #click on Total outstanding value
    Click Element    //div[@id='stats']//div[contains(text(),'Total Outstanding')]/following-sibling::div[*]/a[@class='btn-link']
    #Compare value between DashBoard and report Outstanding
    ${ac_SMA_Outstanding_Total_value}    Get Text    ${ac_report_footer}/tr/td[3]
    ${ac_Member_Outstanding_Total_value}    Get Text    ${ac_report_footer}/tr/td[2]
    Should Be Equal    ${ac_Dashboard_SMA_Outstanding_value}    ${ac_SMA_Outstanding_Total_value}
    Should Be Equal    ${ac_Dashboard_Total_Outstanding_value}    ${ac_Member_Outstanding_Total_value}
    ##End Navigation report Outstanding from Dashboard
    ##Start Navigation report Outstanding from Account Info
    #Go to Account info
    #Super/Master/Agent search Member
    Search Account    ${member}
    ${ac_member_upper}    Evaluate    "${member}".upper()
    Select Frame    ${in_lm_frameAccountInfo}
    #Go to Outstanding report by clicking on outstanding value
    Click Element    //div[@id='tabbalance']//div[contains(text(),'Outstanding')]/following-sibling::div[*]/a[@class='b_nleft']
    #Verify Bet List Running
    Unselect Frame
    Select Frame    main
    Verify Report General    BET LIST (RUNNING) - ${ac_member_upper}    no
    ##Start Super Search Master
    Run Keyword If    '${super}'!='${EMPTY}'    Search Account    ${master}
    #Get outstanding value on leftmenu
    Run Keyword If    '${super}'!='${EMPTY}'    Select Frame    ${in_lm_frameAccountInfo}
    ${ac_Outstanding_value_on_leftmenu}    Run Keyword If    '${super}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    //div[@id='tabbalance']//div[contains(text(),'Outstanding')]/following-sibling::div[*]/a[@class='b_nleft']/font
    #Go to Outstanding report by clicking on outstanding value
    Run Keyword If    '${super}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Element    //div[@id='tabbalance']//div[contains(text(),'Outstanding')]/following-sibling::div[*]/a[@class='b_nleft']
    Run Keyword If    '${super}'!='${EMPTY}'    Unselect Frame
    #Check Breadcrumb shows (supername > mastername)
    Run Keyword If    '${super}'!='${EMPTY}'    Select Frame    main
    Run Keyword If    '${super}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Breadcrumb OutStanding    AGENT OUTSTANDING    ${super}    ${master}    ${agent}
    #Compare value between Left and report Outstanding
    ${ac_Master_Outstanding_Total_value}    Run Keyword If    '${super}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    ${ac_report_footer}/tr/td[3]
    Run Keyword If    '${super}'!='${EMPTY}'    Should Be Equal    ${ac_Outstanding_value_on_leftmenu}    ${ac_Master_Outstanding_Total_value}
    ##End Super Search Master
    ##Start Super/Master search Agent
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Search Account    ${agent}
    #Get outstanding value on leftmenu
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Select Frame    ${in_lm_frameAccountInfo}
    ${ac_Outstanding_value_on_leftmenu}    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    //div[@id='tabbalance']//div[contains(text(),'Outstanding')]/following-sibling::div[*]/a[@class='b_nleft']/font
    #Go to Outstanding report by clicking on outstanding value
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Element    //div[@id='tabbalance']//div[contains(text(),'Outstanding')]/following-sibling::div[*]/a[@class='b_nleft']
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Unselect Frame
    #Check Breadcrumb shows (supername > mastername > agentname)
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Select Frame    main
    Run Keyword If    '${super}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Breadcrumb OutStanding    MEMBER OUTSTANDING    ${super}    ${master}    ${agent}
    Run Keyword If    '${super}'=='${EMPTY}' and '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Breadcrumb OutStanding    MEMBER OUTSTANDING    ${EMPTY}    ${master}    ${agent}
    #Compare value between Left and report Outstanding
    ${ac_Agent_Outstanding_Total_value}    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    ${ac_report_footer}/tr/td[3]
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Should Be Equal    ${ac_Outstanding_value_on_leftmenu}    ${ac_Agent_Outstanding_Total_value}
    ##End Super/Master search Agent
    ##End Navigation report Outstanding from Account Info
    Unselect Frame

Smartphone Verify Report_OutStanding_Check Sort When Drill Down/Up_Outstanding_Column
    [Arguments]    ${downLineLevel}=${EMPTY}    ${master}=${EMPTY}    ${agent}=${EMPTY}    ${SortColumnName}=Member    ${ColumnIndex}=2    ${SortType}=asc
    ...    ${DataType}=numberic
    [Documentation]    This action is used to Check Sort function when drilling down/up of report Outstanding
    ...
    ...    ${downLineLevel}: downline level of account log in
    ...
    ...    ${master}: master username
    ...
    ...    ${agent}: agent username
    ...
    ...    ${SortColumnName}= Master/Agent for First column, Member/Master/Super for Outstanding Column
    ...
    ...    ${ColumnIndex}= Column index of column which need to sort
    ...
    ...    ${SortType}=asc/desc
    ...
    ...    ${DataType}= String/numberic
    ...
    ...    *****History*******
    ...
    ...    2017-03-02: Kingsley created
    Unselect Frame
    select frame    main
    Wait Until Page Not Contains Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    10s    #Wait until BlockUI end
    Smartphone Verify Report General    ${downLineLevel} OUTSTANDING    no
    ${ac_master_upper}    Evaluate    "${master}".upper()
    ${ac_agent_upper}    Evaluate    "${agent}".upper()
    ${ac_super}    Get Substring    ${agent}    \    -4
    ${ac_super_upper}    Evaluate    "${ac_super}".upper()
    ${ac_report_body}    Set Variable    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tbody
    # click submit
    Click Element    ${in_m_btnSubmit}
    #Click on column which want to sort
    : FOR    ${ac_row}    IN RANGE    1    3
    \    ${ac_IsSorted}    Run Keyword And Return Status    Element Should Be Visible    ${in_m_report_table_header_Smartphone}//th[contains(@class,'sorting-${SortType}') and contains(text(),'${SortColumnName}')]
    \    Run Keyword If    '${ac_IsSorted}'=='False'    Click Element By Xpath js    ${in_m_report_table_header_Smartphone}//th[contains(@class,'sorting') and contains(text(),'${SortColumnName}')]
    \    Exit For Loop If    ${ac_IsSorted}
    #count data row of master
    ${ac_NumberOfRow_master}    Get Matching Xpath Count    ${in_m_report_tablebody_Smartphone}/tr
    #check default sort asc Username columns
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}' and ${ac_NumberOfRow_master}>1    Element Should Be Visible    ${in_m_report_table_header_Smartphone}//th[contains(@class,'sorting-${SortType}') and contains(text(),'${SortColumnName}')]    #check icon sort asc show on Outstanding columns
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}' and ${ac_NumberOfRow_master}>1    Check Sort Column Function NormalMode    ${in_m_report_tablebody_Smartphone}    ${ColumnIndex}    ${SortType}    ${DataType}
    # DrillDown to report Agent Outstanding
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Control    ${ac_report_body}//a[@data-custname='${master}' or @data-custname='${ac_master_upper}']
    Wait Until Page Not Contains Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    10s    #Wait until BlockUI end
    ${ColumnIndex}    Run Keyword If    '${SortColumnName}'!='Member' and '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Evaluate    ${ColumnIndex}+1
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    ${ColumnIndex}
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Report General    AGENT OUTSTANDING    no
    #count data row of Agent
    ${ac_NumberOfRow_Agent}    Get Matching Xpath Count    ${in_m_report_tablebody_Smartphone}/tr
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}' and ${ac_NumberOfRow_Agent}>1    Element Should Be Visible    ${in_m_report_table_header_Smartphone}//th[contains(@class,'sorting-${SortType}') and contains(text(),'${SortColumnName}')]    #check icon sort asc show on Outstanding columns
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}' and ${ac_NumberOfRow_Agent}>1    Check Sort Column Function NormalMode    ${in_m_report_tablebody_Smartphone}    ${ColumnIndex}    ${SortType}    ${DataType}
    # DrillDown to report Member Outstanding
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Control    ${ac_report_body}//a[@data-custname='${agent}' or @data-custname='${ac_agent_upper}']
    Wait Until Page Not Contains Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    10s    #Wait until BlockUI end
    ${ColumnIndex}    Run Keyword If    '${SortColumnName}'!='Member' and '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Evaluate    ${ColumnIndex}+1
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    ${ColumnIndex}
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Report General    MEMBER OUTSTANDING    no
    #count data row of Member
    ${ac_NumberOfRow_Member}    Get Matching Xpath Count    ${in_m_report_tablebody_Smartphone}/tr
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}' and '${ac_NumberOfRow_Member}'>1    Element Should Be Visible    ${in_m_report_table_header_Smartphone}//th[contains(@class,'sorting-${SortType}') and contains(text(),'${SortColumnName}')]    #check icon sort asc show on Outstanding columns
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}' and '${ac_NumberOfRow_Member}'>1    Check Sort Column Function NormalMode    ${in_m_report_tablebody_Smartphone}    ${ColumnIndex}    ${SortType}    ${DataType}
    # DrillUp to report Agent Outstanding
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Element By Xpath js    //a[@class='breadcrumb-link' and (contains(text(),'${master}') or contains(text(),'${ac_master_upper}'))]
    Wait Until Page Not Contains Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    10s    #Wait until BlockUI end
    ${ColumnIndex}    Run Keyword If    '${SortColumnName}'!='Member' and '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Evaluate    ${ColumnIndex}-1
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    ${ColumnIndex}
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Report General    AGENT OUTSTANDING    no
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}' and ${ac_NumberOfRow_Agent}>1    Element Should Be Visible    ${in_m_report_table_header_Smartphone}//th[contains(@class,'sorting-${SortType}') and contains(text(),'${SortColumnName}')]    #check icon sort asc show on Outstanding columns
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}' and ${ac_NumberOfRow_Agent}>1    Check Sort Column Function NormalMode    ${in_m_report_tablebody_Smartphone}    ${ColumnIndex}    ${SortType}    ${DataType}
    # DrillUp to report Master Outstanding
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Element By Xpath js    //a[@class='breadcrumb-link' and (contains(text(),'${ac_super}') or contains(text(),'${ac_super_upper}'))]
    Wait Until Page Not Contains Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    10s    #Wait until BlockUI end
    ${ColumnIndex}    Run Keyword If    '${SortColumnName}'!='Member' and '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Evaluate    ${ColumnIndex}-1
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    ${ColumnIndex}
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Report General    MASTER OUTSTANDING    no
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}' and ${ac_NumberOfRow_master}>1    Element Should Be Visible    ${in_m_report_table_header_Smartphone}//th[contains(@class,'sorting-${SortType}') and contains(text(),'${SortColumnName}')]    #check icon sort asc show on Outstanding columns
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}' and ${ac_NumberOfRow_master}>1    Check Sort Column Function NormalMode    ${in_m_report_tablebody_Smartphone}    ${ColumnIndex}    ${SortType}    ${DataType}
    Unselect Frame

    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Report General    AGENT OUTSTANDING    no

Smartphone Verify Report_OutStanding_crosscheck_data
    [Arguments]    ${downLineLevel}=${EMPTY}    ${master}=${EMPTY}    ${agent}=${EMPTY}    ${member}=${EMPTY}
    [Documentation]    This action is used to cross check data of report Outstanding
    ...
    ...    ${downLineLevel}: downline level of account log in
    ...
    ...    ${master}: master username
    ...
    ...    ${agent}: agent username
    ...
    ...    ${member}: member username
    Unselect Frame
    select frame    main
    Smartphone Verify Report General    ${downLineLevel} OUTSTANDING    no
    Verify Report Filter Product List    ${cfProductFilterList}
    ${ac_master_upper}    Evaluate    "${master}".upper()
    ${ac_agent_upper}    Evaluate    "${agent}".upper()
    ${ac_member_upper}    Evaluate    "${member}".upper()
    ${ac_report_body}    Set Variable    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tbody
    ${ac_report_footer}    Set Variable    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tfoot
    # click submit
    Click Element    ${in_m_btnSubmit}
    #Get Super Outstanding and Member Outstanding of report Master Outstanding
    sleep    2s
    ${ac_Super_Outstanding_value}    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    ${ac_report_body}//a[@data-custname='${master}' or @data-custname='${ac_master_upper}']/parent::td/following-sibling::td[2]
    ${ac_Member_Outstanding_value}    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    ${ac_report_body}//a[@data-custname='${master}' or @data-custname='${ac_master_upper}']/parent::td/following-sibling::td[1]
    # DrillDown Master
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Control    ${ac_report_body}//a[@data-custname='${master}' or @data-custname='${ac_master_upper}']
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Report General    AGENT OUTSTANDING    no
    #Get Super and Member Outstanding at total row of report Agent Out Standing.
    ${ac_Super_Outstanding_total_value}    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    ${ac_report_footer}/tr/td[4]
    ${ac_Member_Outstanding_totalvalue}    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    ${ac_report_footer}/tr/td[2]
    #Get Master Outstanding of report Agent Out Standing.
    ${ac_Master_Outstanding_value}    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    ${ac_report_body}//a[@data-custname='${agent}' or @data-custname='${ac_agent_upper}']/parent::td/following-sibling::td[2]
    #Compare with total row after drilldown
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Should Be Equal    ${ac_Super_Outstanding_value}    ${ac_Super_Outstanding_total_value}
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Should Be Equal    ${ac_Member_Outstanding_value}    ${ac_Member_Outstanding_totalvalue}
    #Get Super , Master and Member Outstanding of Report Agent Outstanding
    ${ac_Super_Outstanding_value}    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    ${ac_report_body}//a[@data-custname='${agent}' or @data-custname='${ac_agent_upper}']/parent::td/following-sibling::td[3]
    ${ac_Master_Outstanding_value}    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    ${ac_report_body}//a[@data-custname='${agent}' or @data-custname='${ac_agent_upper}']/parent::td/following-sibling::td[2]
    ${ac_Member_Outstanding_value}    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    ${ac_report_body}//a[@data-custname='${agent}' or @data-custname='${ac_agent_upper}']/parent::td/following-sibling::td[1]
    # DrillDown Agent
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Control    ${ac_report_body}//a[@data-custname='${agent}' or @data-custname='${ac_agent_upper}']
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Report General    MEMBER OUTSTANDING    no
    #Get Super and Member and Master Outstanding at total row of report Member Outstanding.
    ${ac_Super_Outstanding_total_value}    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    ${ac_report_footer}/tr/td[5]
    ${ac_Member_Outstanding_total_value}    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    ${ac_report_footer}/tr/td[2]
    ${ac_Master_Outstanding_total_value}    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    ${ac_report_footer}/tr/td[4]
    #Compare with total row after drilldown
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Should Be Equal    ${ac_Super_Outstanding_value}    ${ac_Super_Outstanding_total_value}
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Should Be Equal    ${ac_Member_Outstanding_value}    ${ac_Member_Outstanding_totalvalue}
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Should Be Equal    ${ac_Master_Outstanding_value}    ${ac_Master_Outstanding_total_value}
    Unselect Frame

    ...

    ...
    ...    ${fromDate}: range data
    ...
    ...    ${toDate}: range data
    ...
    ...    ${master}: account level master
    ...
    ...    ${agent}: account level agent
    ...
    ...    ${member}: account level member
    ...
    ...    ${ac_verifymode}: the way to verify report.
    ...    - username: verify by username
    ...    - 0: verify all row
    ...    - 1+: verify specified row. Eg: 2 means verify the second row

    select frame    main
    Wait Until Page Not Contains Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    10s    #Wait until BlockUI end

    ${ac_toDate_filename}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_ReportType}'=='Current'    Get Value    ${in_m_Report_todate_calendar}

    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}' and ${ac_NumberOfRow}>1    Smartphone Check sort function in report WL detail_Normal mode    Yes    Yes
    ...    ELSE IF    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}' and ${ac_NumberOfRow}>1    Smartphone Check sort function in report WL detail_Normal mode    No    Yes
    ...    ELSE IF    ${ac_NumberOfRow}>1    Smartphone Check sort function in report WL detail_Normal mode    No    No
    Comment    ***get Super account Name using for excel file name***

    ...    A4    ${ac_existData}

Smartphone Verify Report_Win Loss_StackMode
    [Arguments]    ${downLineLevel}=${EMPTY}    ${ac_ReportType}='Current'    ${fromDate}=${EMPTY}    ${toDate}=${EMPTY}    ${master}=${EMPTY}    ${agent}=${EMPTY}
    ...    ${member}=${EMPTY}    ${ac_verifymode}=0
    [Documentation]    This action is used to verify report Winloss and export excel file. Drilldown to calculate report and drill-up by breadcrumb link.
    ...
    ...    ***Step***:
    ...    - Select date range fillter and submit
    ...    - VP: Check the report header of Stackmode
    ...    - VP: Verify Report Filter Product List
    ...    - VP: Verify Filter Date Range if Current report
    ...    - VP: Verify Filter Date Range of History report \ \ \ \ - VP: Check data exist or not and export excel file \ \ \ - VP: Check sort function in report winloss_Stack mode
    ...    - VP: Check Breadcrumb for each level
    ...    - VP: Verify Total Winloss for each level
    ...
    ...    ***Param***:
    ...
    ...    ${downLineLevel}: downline level of account log in
    ...
    ...    ${ac_ReportType}: define Current/History report (Current/History). Default is Current
    ...
    ...    ${fromDate}: range data
    ...
    ...    ${toDate}: range data
    ...
    ...    ${master}: master username
    ...
    ...    ${agent}: agent username
    ...
    ...    ${member}: member username
    ...
    ...    ${ac_verifymode}: the way to verify report.
    ...    - username: verify by username
    ...    - 0: verify all row
    ...    - 1+: verify specified row. Eg: 2 means verify the second row
    Unselect Frame
    select frame    main
    Wait Until Page Not Contains Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    10s    #Wait until BlockUI end
    ${ac_fromDate}    Convert Date Time with Format    ${fromDate}    mm/dd/yyyy
    ${ac_toDate}    Convert Date Time with Format    ${toDate}    mm/dd/yyyy
    sleep    1s

    ${agent_Account}    Set Variable    ${ds_View01_A}

    Close Announcement Popup

    Comment    *****Close Announcement Popup*****

    Wait Until Page Does Not Contain Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    10s

    Should Be Equal    ${ac_text_history_note}    View History Report
    #switch to History report
    Click Element    ${in_m_report_Note_switch_link}
    #Wait until BlockUI end
    Wait Until Page Does Not Contain Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    10s
    Close Announcement Popup
    Select Frame    main

    Wait Until Element Is Visible    ${in_m_report_History_DateRange_form}    10s    Date Range filter of History Report does NOT show.
    ${ac_Fromdate}    Get Element Attribute    ${in_m_report_History_DateRange_dropdown}@data-fromdate
    ${ac_Todate}    Get Element Attribute    ${in_m_report_History_DateRange_dropdown}@data-currentdate
    ${tc_CurrentDate}    Get time    yyyymmdd    UTC- 4 hour
    ${tc_CurrentDate}    Convert Date Time with Format    ${tc_CurrentDate}    mm/dd/yyyy
    Should Be Equal    1    ${ac_Fromdate}
    Should Be Equal    ${tc_CurrentDate}    ${ac_Todate}
    #check Note history report
    ${ac_text_history_note}=    Get Text    ${in_m_report_Note_switch_link}
    Should Be Equal    ${ac_text_history_note}    Back To Current Report
    #switch to Current report
    Click Element    ${in_m_report_Note_switch_link}
    #Wait until BlockUI end
    Wait Until Page Does Not Contain Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    10s
    #check History report Date Range filter layout not show
    Wait Until Element Is Not Visible    ${in_m_report_History_DateRange_form}    10s    Date Range filter of History Report still shows.

Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossAnalysis_AllRow_NormalMode
    [Arguments]    ${ac_ReportTableBody}    ${ac_Level_Login}
    [Documentation]    This action is used to verify below by input report body:
    ...
    ...    1.Total Winloss of the current user (VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM)
    ...    Winloss + Comm = Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    2.Total Winloss of Company (VerifyTotalWinlossCompany)
    ...    Downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company
    ...
    ...
    ...    ${ac_ReportTableBody}: the table body that contains row data need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Login}: Super/Master/Agent
    ## Set variable
    ${ac_boundary_number}    Set Variable    0.02
    ${ac_row_num}    Get Matching Xpath Count    ${ac_ReportTableBody}/tr
    ${ac_EndRow}    Evaluate    ${ac_row_num}+1
    Comment    ****Set Col Total for each level of report Winloss detail****
    ${ac_MemberCol}    Set Variable    6
    ${ac_AgentCol}    Set Variable    9
    ${ac_MasterCol}    Set Variable    12
    ${ac_SuperCol}    Set Variable    15
    ${ac_CompanyCol} =    Set Variable if    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Super'    16    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Master'    13    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Agent'
    ...    10
    : FOR    ${ac_Row}    IN RANGE    1    ${ac_EndRow}
    \    Comment    ***Get row locator***
    \    ${ac_tr}    Set Variable    ${ac_ReportTableBody}/tr[${ac_Row}]
    \    Comment    ****Verify report when level login is Super****
    \    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Super'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Login}    ${ac_CompanyCol}
    \    ...    ${ac_MemberCol}    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${ac_MasterCol}    ${ac_SuperCol}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    \    Comment    ****Verify report when level login is Master****
    \    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Master'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Login}    ${ac_CompanyCol}
    \    ...    ${ac_MemberCol}    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${ac_MasterCol}    ${EMPTY}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    \    Comment    ****Verify report when level login is Agent****
    \    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Agent'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Login}    ${ac_CompanyCol}
    \    ...    ${ac_MemberCol}    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${EMPTY}    ${EMPTY}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    Comment    ***Verify Total row***
    Comment    ****Verify report when level login is Super****
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Super'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}    ${ac_Level_Login}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    ${ac_MemberCol}
    ...    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${ac_MasterCol}    ${ac_SuperCol}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    Comment    ****Verify report when level login is Master****
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Master'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}    ${ac_Level_Login}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    ${ac_MemberCol}
    ...    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${ac_MasterCol}    ${EMPTY}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    Comment    ****Verify report when level login is Agent****
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Agent'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}    ${ac_Level_Login}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    ${ac_MemberCol}
    ...    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${EMPTY}    ${EMPTY}    ${ac_boundary_number}

Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossByRow_AllRow_NormalMode
    [Arguments]    ${ac_RowID}    ${ac_ReportTableBody}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_isDrilldown}=1
    [Documentation]    This action is used to verify below by input RowID:
    ...
    ...    1.Total Winloss of the current user (VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM)
    ...    Winloss + Comm = Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    2.Total Winloss of Company (VerifyTotalWinlossCompany)
    ...    Downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company
    ...
    ...    ${ac_RowID}: the row number that need to check. Start from 1. Input 0 means check all rows.
    ...
    ...    ${ac_ReportTableBody}: the table body that contains row data need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}: the column index of Super/Master/Agent/Member Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Report}: Master/Agent/Member
    ...
    ...    ${ac_isDrilldown}: drill down to downline member or not?
    ## Set variable
    ${ac_boundary_number}    Set Variable    0.02
    ${ac_StartRow}=    Set Variable If    '${ac_RowID}'=='0'    1    ${ac_RowID}
    ${ac_row_num}    Get Matching Xpath Count    ${ac_ReportTableBody}/tr
    ${ac_EndRow}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_RowID}'=='0'    Evaluate    ${ac_row_num}+1
    ...    ELSE    Evaluate    ${ac_RowID}+1
    : FOR    ${ac_Row}    IN RANGE    ${ac_StartRow}    ${ac_EndRow}
    \    ## Set variable
    \    ${ac_tr}    Set Variable    ${ac_ReportTableBody}/tr[${ac_Row}]
    \    ${ac_lnkDownLine}    Set Variable    ${ac_tr}/td[1]/a
    \    ## Check Winloss of SMAM
    \    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    \    ## If report is MEMBER WIN LOSS, check Winloss of Member
    \    ${ac_col_Member_TotalWinloss}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS'    Evaluate    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss} - 3
    \    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS'    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_col_Member_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}
    \    ...    ${ac_boundary_number}
    \    ## Check Winloss of SMAM & Company
    \    Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossCompany_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Check Winloss of SMAM on Total Row
    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Check Winloss of Company on Total Row
    Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossCompany_NormalMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Get report name after drilldown
    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}=    Set Variable if    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS'    AGENT WIN LOSS    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS'    MEMBER WIN LOSS    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS'
    ...    MEMBER BET LIST
    ## Drill down
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1'    Click Control    ${ac_lnkDownLine}
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1' and '${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}'=='MEMBER BET LIST'    Verify Report General    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}    no
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1' and '${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}'!='MEMBER BET LIST'    Smartphone Verify Report General    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}    no

    ...    MEMBER BET LIST

Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossByUsername_NormalMode
    [Arguments]    ${ac_DownlineName}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_isDrilldown}=1
    [Documentation]    This action is used to verify below by input DownlineName:
    ...    1. Total Winloss of the current user (VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM)
    ...    (eg, Winloss + Comm = Total Winloss)
    ...    2.Total Winloss of Company (VerifyTotalWinlossCompany)
    ...    Downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company
    ...
    ...    ${ac_DownlineName}: the username that need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}: the column index of Super/Master/Agent/Member Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Report}: Master/Agent/Member Win loss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_isDrilldown}: drill down to downline member or not?
    ## Set variable
    ${ac_DownlineName_Upper}    Evaluate    "${ac_DownlineName}".upper()
    ${ac_lnkDownline}    Set Variable    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tbody//a[@data-custname='${ac_DownlineName}' or @data-custname='${ac_DownlineName_Upper}']
    ${ac_username_on_web}    Get Text    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tbody//a[@data-custname='${ac_DownlineName}' or @data-custname='${ac_DownlineName_Upper}']
    ${ac_username_on_web}    Trim String    ${ac_username_on_web}
    Should Be Equal    ${ac_username_on_web}    ${ac_DownlineName}
    ${ac_tr}    Set Variable    ${ac_lnkDownline}/parent::td/parent::tr
    ${ac_boundary_number}    Set Variable    0.02
    ## Check Winloss of SMAM
    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## If report is MEMBER WIN LOSS, check Winloss of Member
    ${ac_col_Member_TotalWinloss}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS'    Evaluate    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss} - 3
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS'    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_col_Member_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Check Winloss of Company
    Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossCompany_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Check Winloss of SMAM on Total Row
    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Check Winloss of Company on Total Row
    Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossCompany_NormalMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Get report name after drilldown
    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}=    Set Variable if    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS'    AGENT WIN LOSS    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS'    MEMBER WIN LOSS    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS'
    ...    MEMBER BET LIST
    ## Drill down
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1'    Click Control    ${ac_lnkDownline}
    sleep    1
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1' and '${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}'=='MEMBER BET LIST'    Verify Report General    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}    no
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1' and '${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}'!='MEMBER BET LIST'    Smartphone Verify Report General    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}    no

Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossByUsername_StackMode
    [Arguments]    ${ac_DownlineName}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_isDrilldown}=1
    [Documentation]    This action is used to verify below by input DownlineName:
    ...    1. Total Winloss of the current user (VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM)
    ...    (eg, Winloss + Comm = Total Winloss)
    ...    2.Total Winloss of Company (VerifyTotalWinlossCompany)
    ...    Downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company
    ...
    ...    ${ac_DownlineName}: the username that need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Report}: Master/Agent/Member Win loss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_isDrilldown}: drill down to downline member or not?
    ## Set variable
    ${ac_lnkDownline}    Set Variable    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tbody//a[@data-custname='${ac_DownlineName}']
    ${ac_username_on_web}    Get Text    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tbody//a[@data-custname='${ac_DownlineName}']
    ${ac_username_on_web}    Trim String    ${ac_username_on_web}
    Should Be Equal    ${ac_username_on_web}    ${ac_DownlineName}
    ${ac_tr}    Set Variable    ${ac_lnkDownline}/parent::td/parent::tr
    ${ac_boundary_number}    Set Variable    0.02
    ## Check Winloss of SMAM
    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_StackMode    ${ac_tr}/td[contains(@class,'col-agent-winloss-combined') or contains(@class,'col-master-winloss-combined') or contains(@class,'col-super-winloss-combined')]    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## If report is MEMBER WIN LOSS, check Winloss of Member
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS'    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_StackMode    ${ac_tr}/td[contains(@class,'col-member-winloss-combined')]    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Check Winloss of Company
    Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossCompany_StackMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Check Winloss of SMAM on Total Row
    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_StackMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}/td[contains(@class,'col-agent-winloss-combined') or contains(@class,'col-master-winloss-combined') or contains(@class,'col-super-winloss-combined')]    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## If report is MEMBER WIN LOSS, check Winloss of Member
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS'    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_StackMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}/td[contains(@class,'col-member-winloss-combined')]    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Check Winloss of Company on Total Row
    Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossCompany_StackMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Get report name after drilldown
    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}=    Set Variable if    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS'    AGENT WIN LOSS    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS'    MEMBER WIN LOSS    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS'
    ...    MEMBER BET LIST
    ## Drill down
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1'    Click Control    ${ac_lnkDownline}
    sleep    2
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1' and '${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}'=='MEMBER BET LIST'    Verify Report General    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}    no
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1' and '${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}'!='MEMBER BET LIST'    Smartphone Verify Report General    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}    no

Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossCompany_NormalMode
    [Arguments]    ${ac_Row}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}=0
    [Documentation]    Thisc action is used to verify Total Winloss of Company
    ...    Downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company*(-1)
    ...
    ...    ${ac_row}: the row that contains data need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}: the column index of Super/Master/Agent/Member Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Report}: Master/Agent/Member Winloss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_boundary_number}: the bound of error
    ## Set variable
    ${ac_col_DownlineTotal}    Evaluate    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss} - 3
    ${ac_col_Company}    Evaluate    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss} + 1
    ## get data on web
    ${ac_DownlineTotal_web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_DownlineTotal}]
    ${ac_Company_web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_Company}]
    ${ac_TotalWL_web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_TotalWinloss}]
    ## replace ',' if any
    ${ac_DownlineTotal_web}    Replace String    ${ac_DownlineTotal_web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_Company_web}    Replace String    ${ac_Company_web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_TotalWL_web}    Replace String    ${ac_TotalWL_web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ## Calculate Total WL based on WL and Com displayed on web
    ${ac_Company_Cal}    Evaluate    float(${ac_DownlineTotal_web})+ float(${ac_TotalWL_web})
    ## Get abs of sum(${ac_Company_web} + ${ac_Company_Cal})
    ${ac_Company_Cal_Web_abs}    Evaluate    abs(${ac_Company_web} + ${ac_Company_Cal})
    ## Verify data
    Should Be True    ${ac_Company_Cal_Web_abs}<=${ac_boundary_number}    ${ac_Level_Report} :Downline Total + Current Total Winloss <> Company

Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossCompany_StackMode
    [Arguments]    ${ac_Row}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}=0
    [Documentation]    Thisc action is used to verify Total Winloss of Company
    ...    Downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company*(-1)
    ...
    ...    ${ac_row}: the row that contains data need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Report}: Master/Agent/Member Winloss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_boundary_number}: the bound of error
    ## get data on web
    ${ac_MemberTotal_web}=    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS'    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[contains(@class,'col-member-winloss-combined')]//li[3]
    ${ac_SMATotal_web}=    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'!='MEMBER WIN LOSS'    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[contains(@class,'col-turnover-combined')]//li[3]
    ${ac_DownlineTotal_web}    Set Variable If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS'    ${ac_MemberTotal_web}    ${ac_SMATotal_web}
    ${ac_Company_web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[contains(@class,'col-company')]
    ${ac_TotalWL_web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[contains(@class,'col-agent-winloss-combined') or contains(@class,'col-master-winloss-combined') or contains(@class,'col-super-winloss-combined')]//li[3]
    ## replace ',' if any
    ${ac_DownlineTotal_web}    Replace String    ${ac_DownlineTotal_web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_Company_web}    Replace String    ${ac_Company_web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_TotalWL_web}    Replace String    ${ac_TotalWL_web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ## Calculate Total WL based on WL and Com displayed on web
    ${ac_Company_Cal}    Evaluate    float(${ac_DownlineTotal_web})+ float(${ac_TotalWL_web})
    ## Get abs of sum(${ac_Company_web} + ${ac_Company_Cal})
    ${ac_Company_Cal_Web_abs}    Evaluate    abs(${ac_Company_web} + ${ac_Company_Cal})
    ## Verify data
    Should Be True    ${ac_Company_Cal_Web_abs}<=${ac_boundary_number}    ${ac_Level_Report} :Downline Total + Current Total Winloss <> Company

Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsByRow_AllRow_NormalMode
    [Arguments]    ${ac_RowID}    ${ac_ReportTableBody}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_isDrilldown}=1
    [Documentation]    This action is used to verify below by input report body:
    ...
    ...    1.Total Winloss of the current user (VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM)
    ...    Winloss + Comm = Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    2.Total Winloss of Company (VerifyTotalWinlossCompany)
    ...    Downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company
    ...
    ...    ${ac_RowID}: the row number that need to check. Start from 1. Input 0 means check all rows.
    ...
    ...    ${ac_ReportTableBody}: the table body that contains row data need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Report}: Master/Agent/Member
    ...
    ...    ${ac_isDrilldown}: drill down to downline member or not?
    ## Set variable
    ${ac_boundary_number}    Set Variable    0.02
    ${ac_StartRow}=    Set Variable If    '${ac_RowID}'=='0'    1    ${ac_RowID}
    ${ac_row_num}    Get Matching Xpath Count    ${ac_ReportTableBody}/tr
    ${ac_EndRow}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_RowID}'=='0'    Evaluate    ${ac_row_num}+1
    ...    ELSE    Evaluate    ${ac_RowID}+1
    ## Set Col Total for each level of report Winloss detail
    ${ac_MemberCol}    Set Variable    8
    ${ac_AgentCol}    Set Variable    11
    ${ac_MasterCol}    Set Variable    14
    ${ac_SuperCol}    Set Variable    17
    ${ac_CompanyCol} =    Set Variable if    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    18    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL'    15    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL'
    ...    12
    : FOR    ${ac_Row}    IN RANGE    ${ac_StartRow}    ${ac_EndRow}
    \    ## Get row and link downline
    \    ${ac_tr}    Set Variable    ${ac_ReportTableBody}/tr[${ac_Row}]
    \    ${ac_lnkDownline}    Set Variable    ${ac_tr}/td[1]/a
    \    ## Verify report Master Winloss Detail
    \    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}
    \    ...    ${ac_MemberCol}    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${ac_MasterCol}    ${ac_SuperCol}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    \    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail
    \    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}
    \    ...    ${ac_MemberCol}    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${ac_MasterCol}    ${EMPTY}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    \    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail
    \    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}
    \    ...    ${ac_MemberCol}    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${EMPTY}    ${EMPTY}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Master Winloss Detail on Total Row
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    ${ac_MemberCol}
    ...    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${ac_MasterCol}    ${ac_SuperCol}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail on Total Row
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    ${ac_MemberCol}
    ...    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${ac_MasterCol}    ${EMPTY}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail on Total Row
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    ${ac_MemberCol}
    ...    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${EMPTY}    ${EMPTY}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Get report name after drilldown
    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}=    Set Variable if    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL'    MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL'
    ...    MEMBER BET LIST
    ## Drill down
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1'    Click Control    ${ac_lnkDownline}
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1' and '${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}'=='MEMBER BET LIST'    Verify Report General    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}    no
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1' and '${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}'!='MEMBER BET LIST'    Smartphone Verify Report General    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}    no

Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsByRow_AllRow_StackMode
    [Arguments]    ${ac_RowID}    ${ac_ReportTableBody}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_isDrilldown}=1
    [Documentation]    This action is used to verify below by input report body:
    ...
    ...    1.Total Winloss of the current user (VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM)
    ...    Winloss + Comm = Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    2.Total Winloss of Company (VerifyTotalWinlossCompany)
    ...    Downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company
    ...
    ...    ${ac_RowID}: the row number that need to check. Start from 1. Input 0 means check all rows.
    ...
    ...    ${ac_ReportTableBody}: the table body that contains row data need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Report}: Master/Agent/Member
    ...
    ...    ${ac_isDrilldown}: drill down to downline member or not?
    ## Set variable
    ${ac_boundary_number}    Set Variable    0.02
    ${ac_StartRow}=    Set Variable If    '${ac_RowID}'=='0'    1    ${ac_RowID}
    ${ac_row_num}    Get Matching Xpath Count    ${ac_ReportTableBody}/tr
    ${ac_EndRow}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_RowID}'=='0'    Evaluate    ${ac_row_num}+1
    ...    ELSE    Evaluate    ${ac_RowID}+1
    ${ac_CompanyCol} =    Set Variable if    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    8    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL'    7    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL'
    ...    6
    : FOR    ${ac_Row}    IN RANGE    ${ac_StartRow}    ${ac_EndRow}
    \    ## Get row and link downline
    \    ${ac_tr}    Set Variable    ${ac_ReportTableBody}/tr[${ac_Row}]
    \    ${ac_lnkDownline}    Set Variable    ${ac_tr}/td[1]/a
    \    ## Verify report Master Winloss Detail
    \    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_StackMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}
    \    ...    Yes    Yes    ${ac_boundary_number}
    \    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail
    \    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_StackMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}
    \    ...    No    Yes    ${ac_boundary_number}
    \    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail
    \    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_StackMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}
    \    ...    No    No    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Master Winloss Detail on Total Row
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_StackMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    Yes
    ...    Yes    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail on Total Row
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_StackMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    No
    ...    Yes    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail on Total Row
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_StackMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    No
    ...    No    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Get report name after drilldown
    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}=    Set Variable if    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL'    MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL'
    ...    MEMBER BET LIST
    ## Drill down
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1'    Click Control    ${ac_lnkDownline}
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1' and '${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}'=='MEMBER BET LIST'    Verify Report General    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}    no
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1' and '${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}'!='MEMBER BET LIST'    Smartphone Verify Report General    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}    no

    ...    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${EMPTY}    ${EMPTY}    ${ac_boundary_number}
