    ...    All downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company
    ...
    ...    ${ac_DownlineName}: the username that need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Report}: Master/Agent/Member Win loss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_isDrilldown}: drill down to downline member or not?
    ## Set variable
    ${ac_lnkDownline}    Set Variable    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tbody//a[@data-custname='${ac_DownlineName}']
    ${ac_username_on_web}    Get Text    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tbody//a[@data-custname='${ac_DownlineName}']
    ${ac_username_on_web}    Trim String    ${ac_username_on_web}
    Should Be Equal    ${ac_username_on_web}    ${ac_DownlineName}
    ${ac_tr}    Set Variable    ${ac_lnkDownline}/parent::td/parent::tr
    ${ac_boundary_number}    Set Variable    0.02
    ## Set Col Total for each level of report Winloss detail
    ${ac_CompanyCol} =    Set Variable if    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    8    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL'    7    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL'
    ...    6
    ## Verify report Master Winloss Detail
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_StackMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    Yes
    ...    Yes    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Master Winloss Detail on Total Row
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_StackMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    Yes
    ...    Yes    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_StackMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    No
    ...    Yes    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail on Total Row
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_StackMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    No
    ...    Yes    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_StackMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    No
    ...    No    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail on Total Row
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_StackMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow_Smartphone}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    No
    ...    No    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Get report name after drilldown
    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}=    Set Variable if    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL'    MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL'
    ...    MEMBER BET LIST
    ## Drill down
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1'    Click Element    ${ac_lnkDownline}
    sleep    2
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1' and '${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}'=='MEMBER BET LIST'    Verify Report General    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}    no
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1' and '${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}'!='MEMBER BET LIST'    Smartphone Verify Report General    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}    no

Sum Total data of match winloss details by column_NormalMode
    [Arguments]    ${ac_LevelLogin}
    [Documentation]    This keyword to sum data of every match by column and compare with grand total row
    ...
    ...    ***Step***
    ...
    ...    1. Get total data row
    ...
    ...    2. Declare sum variable for every column
    ...
    ...    3. Sum data from every row in match
    ...
    ...    4. Get row Grand Total
    ...
    ...    5. Compare Sum data with Grand Total row
    ...
    ...    ***Param***
    ...
    ...    ${ac_LevelLogin}: input level of login account (Super/Master/Agent)
    ...
    ...    ******History******
    ...
    ...    2017/04/25: Kingsley created
    Wait Until Page Not Contains Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    10s    #Wait until BlockUI end
    Comment    ***1. Get total data row ***
    ${ac_TotalDataRow}    Get Matching Xpath Count    //table[@id='tbl-report']//tbody//tr[@class='' or @class='even-row']
    ${ac_TotalDataRow}    Evaluate    ${ac_TotalDataRow}+1
    ${ac_TotalDataRow}    Convert To Number    ${ac_TotalDataRow}
    Comment    ***2. Declare sum variable for every column***
    ${ac_Sum_Turnover}    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_Sum_GrossComm}    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_Sum_MemberWL}    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_Sum_MemberComm}    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_Sum_MemberTotal}    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_Sum_AgentWL}    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_Sum_AgentComm}    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_Sum_AgentTotal}    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_Sum_MasterWL}    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_Sum_MasterComm}    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_Sum_MasterTotal}    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_Sum_SuperWL}    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_Sum_SuperComm}    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_Sum_SuperTotal}    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_CompanyCommCol} =    Set Variable if    '${ac_LevelLogin}'=='Super'    16    '${ac_LevelLogin}'=='Master'    13    '${ac_LevelLogin}'=='Agent'
    ...    10
    ${ac_CompanyTotalCol} =    Set Variable if    '${ac_LevelLogin}'=='Super'    17    '${ac_LevelLogin}'=='Master'    14    '${ac_LevelLogin}'=='Agent'
    ...    11
    ${ac_Sum_CompanyComm}    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_Sum_CompanyTotal}    Set Variable    0
    Comment    ***3. Get data from every row in match***
    : FOR    ${ac_iRow}    IN RANGE    1    ${ac_TotalDataRow}
    \    ${ac_DataRow}    Set Variable    //table[@id='tbl-report']//tbody//tr[not(@class='tbl-footer' or @class='match-title-row' or @class='sub-total-row')]
    \    ${ac_iRow_Locator}    Set Variable    ${ac_DataRow}[${ac_iRow}]
    \    Comment    ***Turnover column***
    \    ${ac_Turnover}    Get Text    ${ac_iRow_Locator}/td[2]
    \    ${ac_Turnover}    Replace String    ${ac_Turnover}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    \    ${ac_Sum_Turnover}    Evaluate    ${ac_Sum_Turnover}+${ac_Turnover}
    \    Comment    ***Member column***
    \    ${ac_GrossComm}    Get Text    ${ac_iRow_Locator}/td[3]
    \    ${ac_GrossComm}    Replace String    ${ac_GrossComm}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    \    ${ac_SumGrossComm}    Evaluate    ${ac_SumGrossComm}+${ac_GrossComm}
    \    Comment    ***Member column***
    \    ${ac_MemberWL}    Get Text    ${ac_iRow_Locator}/td[4]
    \    ${ac_MemberWL}    Replace String    ${ac_MemberWL}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    \    ${ac_Sum_MemberWL}    Evaluate    ${ac_Sum_MemberWL}+${ac_MemberWL}
    \    ${ac_MemberComm}    Get Text    ${ac_iRow_Locator}/td[5]
    \    ${ac_MemberComm}    Replace String    ${ac_MemberComm}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    \    ${ac_Sum_MemberComm}    Evaluate    ${ac_Sum_MemberComm}+${ac_MemberComm}
    \    ${ac_MemberTotal}    Get Text    ${ac_iRow_Locator}/td[6]
    \    ${ac_MemberTotal}    Replace String    ${ac_MemberTotal}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    \    ${ac_Sum_MemberTotal}    Evaluate    ${ac_Sum_MemberTotal}+${ac_MemberTotal}
    \    Comment    ***Agent column***
    \    ${ac_AgentWL}    Get Text    ${ac_iRow_Locator}/td[7]
    \    ${ac_AgentWL}    Replace String    ${ac_AgentWL}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    \    ${ac_Sum_AgentWL}    Evaluate    ${ac_Sum_AgentWL}+${ac_AgentWL}
    \    ${ac_AgentComm}    Get Text    ${ac_iRow_Locator}/td[8]
    \    ${ac_AgentComm}    Replace String    ${ac_AgentComm}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    \    ${ac_Sum_AgentComm}    Evaluate    ${ac_Sum_AgentComm}+${ac_AgentComm}
    \    ${ac_AgentTotal}    Get Text    ${ac_iRow_Locator}/td[9]
    \    ${ac_AgentTotal}    Replace String    ${ac_AgentTotal}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    \    ${ac_Sum_AgentTotal}    Evaluate    ${ac_Sum_AgentTotal}+${ac_AgentTotal}
    \    Comment    ***Master column***
    \    ${ac_MasterWL}    Run Keyword If    '${LevelLogin}'=='Master' or '${LevelLogin}'=='Super'    Get Text    ${ac_iRow_Locator}/td[10]
    \    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0
    \    ${ac_MasterWL}    Replace String    ${ac_MasterWL}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    \    ${ac_Sum_MasterWL}    Evaluate    ${ac_Sum_MasterWL}+${ac_MasterWL}
    \    ${ac_MasterComm}    Run Keyword If    '${LevelLogin}'=='Master' or '${LevelLogin}'=='Super'    Get Text    ${ac_iRow_Locator}/td[11]
    \    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0
    \    ${ac_MasterComm}    Replace String    ${ac_MasterComm}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    \    ${ac_Sum_MasterComm}    Evaluate    ${ac_Sum_MasterComm}+${ac_MasterComm}
    \    ${ac_MasterTotal}    Run Keyword If    '${LevelLogin}'=='Master' or '${LevelLogin}'=='Super'    Get Text    ${ac_iRow_Locator}/td[12]
    \    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0
    \    ${ac_MasterTotal}    Replace String    ${ac_MasterTotal}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    \    ${ac_Sum_MasterTotal}    Evaluate    ${ac_Sum_MasterTotal}+${ac_MasterTotal}
    \    Comment    ***Super column***
    \    ${ac_SuperWL}    Run Keyword If    '${LevelLogin}'=='Super'    Get Text    ${ac_iRow_Locator}/td[13]
    \    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0
    \    ${ac_SuperWL}    Replace String    ${ac_SuperWL}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    \    ${ac_Sum_SuperWL}    Evaluate    ${ac_Sum_SuperWL}+${ac_SuperWL}
    \    ${ac_SuperComm}    Run Keyword If    '${LevelLogin}'=='Super'    Get Text    ${ac_iRow_Locator}/td[14]
    \    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0
    \    ${ac_SuperComm}    Replace String    ${ac_SuperComm}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    \    ${ac_Sum_SuperComm}    Evaluate    ${ac_Sum_SuperComm}+${ac_SuperComm}
    \    ${ac_SuperTotal}    Run Keyword If    '${LevelLogin}'=='Super'    Get Text    ${ac_iRow_Locator}/td[15]
    \    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0
    \    ${ac_SuperTotal}    Replace String    ${ac_SuperTotal}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    \    ${ac_Sum_SuperTotal}    Evaluate    ${ac_Sum_SuperTotal}+${ac_SuperTotal}
    \    Comment    ***Company column***
    \    ${ac_CompanyComm}    Get Text    ${ac_iRow_Locator}/td[${ac_CompanyCommCol}]
    \    ${ac_CompanyComm}    Replace String    ${ac_CompanyComm}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    \    ${ac_Sum_CompanyComm}    Evaluate    ${ac_Sum_CompanyComm}+${ac_CompanyComm}
    \    ${ac_CompanyTotal}    Get Text    ${ac_iRow_Locator}/td[${ac_CompanyTotalCol}]
    \    ${ac_CompanyTotal}    Replace String    ${ac_CompanyTotal}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    \    ${ac_Sum_CompanyTotal}    Evaluate    ${ac_Sum_CompanyTotal}+${ac_CompanyTotal}
    Comment    ***4. Get row Grand Total***
    ${ac_GrandRow}    Set Variable    //table[@id='tbl-report']//tbody//tr[@class='tbl-footer']
    ${ac_GrandTurnover}    Get Text    ${ac_GrandRow}/td[2]
    ${ac_GrandTurnover}    Replace String    ${ac_GrandTurnover}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_GrandGrossComm}    Get Text    ${ac_GrandRow}/td[3]
    ${ac_GrandGrossComm}    Replace String    ${ac_GrandGrossComm}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    Comment    ***Grand Member column***
    ${ac_GrandMemberWL}    Get Text    ${ac_GrandRow}/td[4]
    ${ac_GrandMemberWL}    Replace String    ${ac_GrandMemberWL}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_GrandMemberComm}    Get Text    ${ac_GrandRow}/td[5]
    ${ac_GrandMemberComm}    Replace String    ${ac_GrandMemberComm}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    Comment    ${ac_GrandMemberComm}    Convert To Number    ${ac_GrandMemberComm}
    ${ac_GrandMemberTotal}    Get Text    ${ac_GrandRow}/td[6]
    ${ac_GrandMemberTotal}    Replace String    ${ac_GrandMemberTotal}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    Comment    ***Grand Agent column***
    ${ac_GrandAgentWL}    Get Text    ${ac_GrandRow}/td[7]
    ${ac_GrandAgentWL}    Replace String    ${ac_GrandAgentWL}    ,    ${EMPTY}

    ${ac_GrandAgentComm}    Replace String    ${ac_GrandAgentComm}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_GrandAgentTotal}    Get Text    ${ac_GrandRow}/td[9]
    ${ac_GrandAgentTotal}    Replace String    ${ac_GrandAgentTotal}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    Comment    ***Grand Master column***
    ${ac_GrandMasterWL}    Run Keyword If    '${LevelLogin}'=='Master' or '${LevelLogin}'=='Super'    Get Text    ${ac_GrandRow}/td[10]
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0

    ${ac_GrandMasterComm}    Run Keyword If    '${LevelLogin}'=='Master' or '${LevelLogin}'=='Super'    Get Text    ${ac_GrandRow}/td[11]
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_GrandMasterComm}    Replace String    ${ac_GrandMasterComm}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_GrandMasterTotal}    Run Keyword If    '${LevelLogin}'=='Master' or '${LevelLogin}'=='Super'    Get Text    ${ac_GrandRow}/td[12]
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_GrandMasterTotal}    Replace String    ${ac_GrandMasterTotal}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    Comment    ***Grand Super column***
    ${ac_GrandSuperWL}    Run Keyword If    '${LevelLogin}'=='Super'    Get Text    ${ac_GrandRow}/td[13]
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_GrandSuperWL}    Replace String    ${ac_GrandSuperWL}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_GrandSuperComm}    Run Keyword If    '${LevelLogin}'=='Super'    Get Text    ${ac_GrandRow}/td[14]
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_GrandSuperComm}    Replace String    ${ac_GrandSuperComm}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_GrandSuperTotal}    Run Keyword If    '${LevelLogin}'=='Super'    Get Text    ${ac_GrandRow}/td[15]
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_GrandSuperTotal}    Replace String    ${ac_GrandSuperTotal}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    Comment    ***Grand Company column***
    ${ac_GrandCompanyComm}    Get Text    ${ac_GrandRow}/td[${ac_CompanyCommCol}]
    ${ac_GrandCompanyComm}    Replace String    ${ac_GrandCompanyComm}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_GrandCompanyTotal}    Get Text    ${ac_GrandRow}/td[${ac_CompanyTotalCol}]
    ${ac_GrandCompanyTotal}    Replace String    ${ac_GrandCompanyTotal}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    Comment    ***5. Compare Sum data with Grand Total row***
    Compare value with boundary range    ${ac_Sum_Turnover}    ${ac_GrandTurnover}    0.5
    Compare value with boundary range    ${ac_Sum_GrossComm}    ${ac_GrandGrossComm}    0.5
    Compare value with boundary range    ${ac_Sum_MemberWL}    ${ac_GrandMemberWL}    0.5
    Compare value with boundary range    ${ac_Sum_MemberComm}    ${ac_GrandMemberComm}    0.5
    Compare value with boundary range    ${ac_Sum_MemberTotal}    ${ac_GrandMemberTotal}    0.5

    Wait Until Element Is Visible    ${in_m_report_lstBreadcrums}    ${cfWaitControl}
    ${ac_actBreadcrumb}    get text    ${in_m_report_lstBreadcrums}
    Run Keyword And Continue On Failure    Should Be Equal    ${ac_actBreadcrumb}    ${exBreakcrumb}
    Comment    ${ac_actBreadcrumbList}    Split String    ${ac_actBreadcrumb}    ${SPACE}
    Comment    ${n}    Get Length    ${expectedNodeList}
    Comment    ${n}    Evaluate    ${n}-1
    Comment    : FOR    ${i}    IN RANGE    0    ${n}
    Comment    \    Run Keyword And Continue On Failure    Should Be Equal    @{ac_actBreadcrumbList}[${i}]    @{expectedNodeList}[${i}]    ${issueID}

Verify Report Filter Date Range
    [Arguments]    ${ListDateRange}
    [Documentation]    This action to Verify Date Range List in Date Range dropdown list on Web by input list of Date Range separated by ','
    ...
    ...    Ex:
    ...    *Report Winloss Detail*: Today,Yesterday,This Week,Last Week,This Month,Last Month,Since Last Month,Specific Dates
    ...
    ...    Param:
    ...
    ...    ${ListDateRange}
    ...
    ...    *********History***********
    ...
    ...    2016-12-16: Kingsley
    Unselect Frame
    select frame    main
    ${ac_daterange_expected}    Set Variable    ${ListDateRange}
    Wait Until Page Not Contains Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    10s    #Wait until BlockUI end
    Wait Until Element Is Enabled    ${in_m_report_DateRange_dropdown_list_button}    10s
    Click Element    ${in_m_report_DateRange_dropdown_list_button}
    ${ac_text}    Get Text    ${in_m_report_DateRange_dropdown_list}
    ${ac_text}    Replace String    ${ac_text}    \n    ,    -1
    Should Be Equal    ${ac_daterange_expected}    ${ac_text}
    Click Element    ${in_m_report_DateRange_dropdown_list_button}    # Click dropdown list again to close it

Verify Report Filter Product List
    [Arguments]    ${ListProduct}
    [Documentation]    This action to Verify Product List in Product dropdown list on Web by input list of product separated by ','
    ...
    ...    Ex:
    ...    *Report Winloss, Winloss Detail, Daily Winloss Analysis, Win Loss Analysis Chart*: All Products (@numberofproduct@),Select all,Sportsbook,Racing,Number Game,Live Casino,Virtual Sports,Casino,Keno,Gold Deluxe,Colossus Bets,Allbet,Macau Games,Fishing World,Lottery
    ...
    ...    *Match Winloss details*: @numberofproduct@ selected, All Products, Sportbook, Racing, Number Game, Virtual Sports
    ...
    ...    Param:
    ...
    ...    ${ListProduct}
    ...
    ...    *********History***********
    ...
    ...    2016-12-16: Kingsley
    ...
    ...    *******List of TCs are automated*********:
    ...
    ...    Alpha-6020:Verify product checkbox (Current Report)
    ...
    ...    Alpha-5997:Verify product checkbox (History Report)
    Unselect Frame
    select frame    main
    ${ac_product_expected}    Set Variable    ${ListProduct}
    Wait Until Keyword Succeeds    1m    2s    Wait Until Page Not Contains Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    10s    #Wait until BlockUI end
    Close Announcement Popup
    Close Net TurnOver Tool tip
    Capture Page Screenshot
    Wait Until Element Is Enabled    ${in_m_report_Product_dropdown_list_button}    10s
    Click Element    ${in_m_report_Product_dropdown_list_button}
    Capture Page Screenshot
    #Get text of all products show on product dropdown list
    Wait Until Element Is Visible    ${in_m_report_Report_filter_form}//div[@class='btn-group open']    10s
    ${ac_text}    Get Text    ${in_m_report_Report_filter_form}//div[@class='btn-group open']
    ${ac_text}    Replace String    ${ac_text}    \n    ,    -1
    #Get number of product show on product dropdown list
    ${ac_option_number}    Get Matching Xpath Count    ${in_m_report_Product_dropdown_list}//input
    ${ac_option_number}    Evaluate    ${ac_option_number}-1
    ${ac_option_number}    Convert To String    ${ac_option_number}
    ${ac_product_expected}    Replace String    ${ac_product_expected}    @NumberOfProduct@    ${ac_option_number}
    Should Be Equal    ${ac_product_expected}    ${ac_text}
    Click Element    ${in_m_report_Product_dropdown_list_button}    # Click dropdown list again to close it

Verify Report General
    [Arguments]    ${expectedTile}=${EMPTY}    ${verifySumTotal}=yes
    ## Set variable
    Wait Until Element Is Visible    ${in_m_report_divReportTitle}    ${cfWaitControl}
    Wait Until Keyword Succeeds    1 min    2s    Element Should Contain    ${in_m_report_divReportTitle}    ${expectedTile}
    ${ac_sum}    Run Keyword If    '${verifySumTotal}'=='yes'    Run Keyword And Continue On Failure    Execute Javascript    ${init_gbWorksSpace}\\Actions\\JavaScript\\Report.js
    Run Keyword If    '${verifySumTotal}'=='yes'    Run Keyword And Continue On Failure    Should Be Equal As Strings    ${ac_sum}    1    SUM VALUE IS WRONG

Verify Report Header Data
    [Arguments]    ${ac_group}    ${ac_reportlevel}    ${ac_rptName}    ${ac_header}    ${ac_tabledata}    ${isClickSubmit}=0
    [Documentation]    This action is used to check header of report and table data controls displayed on web
    ...
    ...    ${ac_group} : the group that contain report (TOTAL BETS, FORECAST,...)
    ...
    ...    ${ac_reportlevel}: the level of report (Master/Agent/Member)
    ...
    ...    ${ac_rptName}: the report link name
    ...
    ...    ${ac_header}: the report header
    ...
    ...    ${ac_tabledata}: the control of table data
    ...
    ...    ${isClickSubmit}: need to click Submit button or not (1/0)
    Select Left Menu    ${ac_group}    ${ac_rptName}
    ## Set variable
    Unselect Frame
    select frame    main
    ${ac_headerByLevel}=    Replace String    ${ac_header}    Master    ${ac_reportlevel}
    Run Keyword And Continue On Failure    Wait Until Element Is Visible    ${ac_headerByLevel}    ${cfWaitControl}
    Run Keyword If    '${isClickSubmit}'=='1'    Click Element    ${in_Reports_btnSubmit}
    Run Keyword And Continue On Failure    Wait Until Element Is Visible    ${ac_tabledata}    ${cfWaitControl}
    Unselect Frame

Verify Report_Cancelled Bets
    Unselect Frame
    select frame    main
    Verify Report General    VOID/REJECTED BET LIST (LAST 3 DAYS)    no

    Run Keyword If    ${ac_existData}    Report_VerifyFunctionExportExcel    ${in_Reports_btnExportToExcel_smartphone}    VOID/REJECTED BET LIST (LAST 3 DAYS)    Cancelled Bets    Sheet 1
    ...    A1    True
    Unselect Frame

Verify Report_OutStanding
    [Arguments]    ${downLineLevel}=${EMPTY}    ${master}=${EMPTY}    ${agent}=${EMPTY}    ${member}=${EMPTY}
    [Documentation]    This action is used to verify report *Outstanding*. (Smoke test)
    ...
    ...    ***Step***:
    ...
    ...    - 1 Verify Report name
    ...    - 2 Drill down to downline
    ...
    ...    ***Param***:
    ...
    ...    ${downLineLevel}: downline level of account log in
    ...
    ...    ${master}: master username
    ...
    ...    ${agent}: agent username
    ...
    ...    ${member}: member username
    Unselect Frame
    select frame    main
    Wait Until Page Not Contains Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    ${cfWaitControl}    #Wait until BlockUI end
    Comment    ******1 VP: Verify Report Name******
    Smartphone Verify Report General    ${downLineLevel} OUTSTANDING    no
    Comment    ***Drill down to Agent Outstanding***
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Control    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tbody//a[@data-custname='${master}']
    Wait Until Page Not Contains Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    ${cfWaitControl}    #Wait until BlockUI end
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Report General    AGENT OUTSTANDING    no
    Comment    ***Drill down to Member Outstanding***
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Control    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tbody//a[@data-custname='${agent}']
    Wait Until Page Not Contains Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    ${cfWaitControl}    #Wait until BlockUI end
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Verify Report General    MEMBER OUTSTANDING    no
    Comment    ***Drill down to Member BetList***
    Run Keyword If    '${member}'!='${EMPTY}'    Click Control    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tbody//a[@data-custname='${member}']
    Wait Until Page Not Contains Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    ${cfWaitControl}    #Wait until BlockUI end
    Run Keyword If    '${member}'!='${EMPTY}'    Verify Report General    BET LIST    no
    Unselect Frame

Verify Report_Results
    [Arguments]    ${selectedDate}=${EMPTY}
    [Documentation]    ${selectedDate}: 'today', 'yesterday', <specialDate>
    Unselect Frame
    select frame    main
    # Verify Result: NORMAL
    Verify Report General    RESULTS    no
    Run Keyword If    '${selectedDate}'=='yesterday'    click control    ${in_m_rptOld_btnYesterday}
    Run Keyword If    '${selectedDate}'=='today'    click control    ${in_m_rptOld_btnToday}
    Verify Report General    RESULTS    no
    ${ac_lstSport}    Run Keyword And Continue On Failure    Get List Items    ${in_m_rptOld_cboSport}
    ${ac_sportLen}    Get Matching Xpath Count    ${in_m_rptOld_cboSport}//option
    ${index}    Evaluate    ${ac_sportLen}-1
    Run Keyword And Continue On Failure    Select From List By Label    ${in_m_rptOld_cboSport}    ${ac_lstSport[${index}]}
    Verify Report General    RESULTS    no
    ${ac_lstLeague}    Run Keyword And Continue On Failure    Get List Items    ${in_m_rptOld_cboLeague}
    ${ac_leagueLen}    Get Matching Xpath Count    ${in_m_rptOld_cboLeague}//option
    ${index}    Evaluate    ${ac_leagueLen}-1
    Run Keyword And Continue On Failure    Select From List By Label    ${in_m_rptOld_cboLeague}    ${ac_lstLeague[${index}]}
    Verify Report General    RESULTS    no
    Comment    # Verify Result: Outright
    Click Control    ${in_m_btnOutright}
    Verify Report General    RESULTS    no

    Verify Report General    RESULTS    no

Verify Report_Win Loss Detail_ExportExcel
    [Arguments]    ${downLineLevel}=${EMPTY}    ${ac_ReportType}='Current'    ${fromDate}=${EMPTY}    ${toDate}=${EMPTY}    ${master}=${EMPTY}    ${agent}=${EMPTY}
    ...    ${member}=${EMPTY}    ${ac_verifymode}=0
    [Documentation]    This action is used to verify report Winloss details and export excel file
    ...
    ...    ***Step***:
    ...
    ...    - 1 Select date range fillter and submit
    ...    - 2 VP: Verify Report Name
    ...    - 3 VP: Check data exist or not and export excel file
    ...    - 4 VP: Verify Total Winloss Detail for each level. User can use some action below:
    ...
    ...    a. VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsByUsername : This action is used to verify below by input DownlineName
    ...
    ...    b. VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsByRow_AllRow: This action is used to verify below by input RowID or "All" if you want to check all row
    ...
    ...    ***Param***:
    ...
    ...    ${downLineLevel}: downline level of account log in
    ...
    ...    ${fromDate}: range data
    ...
    ...    ${toDate}: range data
    ...
    ...    ${master}: account level master
    ...
    ...    ${agent}: account level agent
    ...
    ...    ${member}: account level member
    ...
    ...    ${ac_verifymode}: the way to verify report.
    ...    - username: verify by username
    ...    - 0: verify all row
    ...    - 1+: verify specified row. Eg: 2 means verify the second row
    Unselect Frame
    select frame    main
    Wait Until Page Not Contains Element    ${in_m_report_blockUI}    10s    #Wait until BlockUI end
    ${ac_fromDate}    Convert Date Time with Format    ${fromDate}    mm/dd/yyyy
    ${ac_toDate}    Convert Date Time with Format    ${toDate}    mm/dd/yyyy
    sleep    1s
    Comment    Run Keyword And Continue On Failure    Wait Until Element Is Not Visible    //*[@id='menuPopupAd']/div[2]    ${cfWaitControl}
    ${agent_Account}    Set Variable    ${ds_View01_A}
    Comment    *****Close Pop up*****
    Comment    Close Announcement Popup
    Comment    select frame    main
    Comment    *****1 Select date range fillter and submit*****
    Smartphone Friendly Select Report Filter    ${ac_fromDate}    ${ac_toDate}    ${EMPTY}    ${EMPTY}
    Click Element    ${in_m_btnSubmit}
    Comment    *****Close Pop up*****
    Comment    Close Announcement Popup
    Comment    @{main}    main
    ${ac_fromDate_filename}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_ReportType}'=='Current'    Get Value    ${in_m_Report_fromdate_calendar}
    ...    ELSE    Get Element Attribute    ${in_m_Report_History_datetime_dropdown}@data-dateinmonth
    ${ac_toDate_filename}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_ReportType}'=='Current'    Get Value    ${in_m_Report_todate_calendar}
    ...    ELSE    Get Element Attribute    ${in_m_Report_History_datetime_dropdown}@data-currentdate
    ${ac_fromDate_filename}    Replace String    ${ac_fromDate_filename}    /    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_toDate_filename}    Replace String    ${ac_toDate_filename}    /    ${EMPTY}
    Comment    ******2 VP: Verify Report Name******
    Smartphone Verify Report General    ${downLineLevel} WIN LOSS DETAIL    no
    Comment    *****3 VP: Check data exist or not and export excel file********
    ${ac_existData}    Run Keyword And Return Status    Element Should Be Visible    //table[@id='tbl-report']/tbody/tr[1]/td[1]/a
    Comment    ***get Super account Name using for excel file name***
    ${ac_super_excel_filename}    Get Substring    ${agent_Account}    \    -4
    ${ac_super_excel_filename}    Evaluate    "${ac_super_excel_filename}".upper()
    Comment    ***Export Excel Super***
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Report_VerifyFunctionExportExcel    ${in_Reports_btnExportToExcel_smartphone}    MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL    Win Loss Detail-${ac_super_excel_filename}-${ac_fromDate_filename}-${ac_toDate_filename}    Sheet 1
    ...    A4    ${ac_existData}
    Comment    ***4 VP: Verify Total Winloss Detail for each level***
    Run Keyword If    '${master}'!='${EMPTY}' and '${ac_verifymode}'=='username'    Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsByUsername_NormalMode    ${master}    MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL    1
    Comment    ***get Master account Name using for excel file name***
    ${ac_master_excel_filename}    Get Substring    ${agent_Account}    \    -2
    ${ac_master_excel_filename_upper}    Evaluate    "${ac_master_excel_filename}".upper()
    Comment    ***Export Excel Master***
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Report_VerifyFunctionExportExcel    ${in_Reports_btnExportToExcel_smartphone}    AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL    Win Loss Detail-${ac_master_excel_filename_upper}-${ac_fromDate_filename}-${ac_toDate_filename}    Sheet 1
    ...    A4    ${ac_existData}
    Comment    ***Verify TotalWinloss when level login is Master***
    Run Keyword If    '${agent}'!='${EMPTY}' and '${ac_verifymode}'=='username'    Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsByUsername_NormalMode    ${agent}    AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL    1
    Comment    ***Export Excel Agent***
    Run Keyword If    '${member}'!='${EMPTY}'    Smartphone Report_VerifyFunctionExportExcel    ${in_Reports_btnExportToExcel_smartphone}    MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL    Win Loss Detail-${agent_Account}-${ac_fromDate_filename}-${ac_toDate_filename}    Sheet 1
    ...    A4    ${ac_existData}
    Comment    ***Verify TotalWinloss when level login is Agent***
    Run Keyword If    '${member}'!='${EMPTY}' and '${ac_verifymode}'=='username'    Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsByUsername_NormalMode    ${member}    MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL    1
    Comment    ****** 5 VP: Verify Function Export Excel Betlist microsite*******
    Wait Until Element Is Visible    ${in_m_report_divReportTitle}    10s
    ${ac_reportHeader}    Get Text    ${in_m_report_divReportTitle}
    ${ac_BetlistExcelFileName}    Replace String    ${ac_reportHeader}    >    ${EMPTY}

VerifyTotalMatchWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode
    [Arguments]    ${ac_Row}    ${ac_Level_Login}    ${ac_col_Company}    ${ac_col_MemTotal}    ${ac_col_AgentTotal}    ${ac_col_MasterTotal}=${EMPTY}
    ...    ${ac_col_SuperTotal}=${EMPTY}    ${ac_boundary_number}=0
    [Documentation]    Thisc action is used to verify Total Winloss of Company in report *Match Win Loss Details*
    ...
    ...    All downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company
    ...    Eg:
    ...    - Master Winloss Detail report: Member total + Agent Total + Master Total + Super Total = Company*(-1)
    ...    - Agent Winloss Detail report: Member total + Agent Total + Master Total = Company*(-1)
    ...    - Member Winloss Detail report: Member total + Agent Total = Company*(-1)
    ...
    ...    ${ac_row}: the row that contains data need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Report}: Master/Agent/Member Win loss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_Company}: the column index of Company
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_MemTotal}: the column index of Member Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_AgentTotal}: the column index of Agent Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_MasterTotal}: the column index of Master Total Winloss. It could be empty.
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_SuperTotal}: the column index of Super Total Winloss. It could be empty.
    ...
    ...    ${ac_boundary_number}: the bound of error
    Comment    ## Set variable
    Comment    ${ac_boundary_number}    Set Variable    0.02
    ## get data on web
    ${ac_MemberTotal_Web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_MemTotal}]
    ${ac_AgentTotal_Web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_AgentTotal}]
    ${ac_MasterTotal_Web}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_col_MasterTotal}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_MasterTotal}]
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_SuperTotal_Web}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_col_SuperTotal}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_SuperTotal}]
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_Company_web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_Company}]
    ## replace ',' if any
    ${ac_MemberTotal_Web}    Replace String    ${ac_MemberTotal_Web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_AgentTotal_Web}    Replace String    ${ac_AgentTotal_Web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_MasterTotal_Web}    Replace String    ${ac_MasterTotal_Web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_SuperTotal_Web}    Replace String    ${ac_SuperTotal_Web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_Company_web}    Replace String    ${ac_Company_web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ## Calculate Total WL based on WL and Com displayed on web
    ${ac_Company_Cal}    Evaluate    ${ac_MemberTotal_Web} + ${ac_AgentTotal_Web} + ${ac_MasterTotal_Web} + ${ac_SuperTotal_Web}
    ## Get abs of sum(${ac_Company_web} + ${ac_Company_Cal})
    ${ac_Company_Cal_Web_abs}    Evaluate    abs(${ac_Company_web} + ${ac_Company_Cal})
    ## Verify data
    Should Be True    ${ac_Company_Cal_Web_abs}<=${ac_boundary_number}    ${ac_Level_Login} : All downline Total + Current Total Winloss <> Company
    ## Check Winloss of SMAM
    #Member
    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${ac_Row}    ${ac_col_MemTotal}    ${ac_Level_Login}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    #Agent
    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${ac_Row}    ${ac_col_AgentTotal}    ${ac_Level_Login}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    #Master
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_col_MasterTotal}'!='${EMPTY}'    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${ac_Row}    ${ac_col_MasterTotal}    ${ac_Level_Login}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    #Super
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_col_SuperTotal}'!='${EMPTY}'    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${ac_Row}    ${ac_col_SuperTotal}    ${ac_Level_Login}    ${ac_boundary_number}

VerifyTotalMatchWinlossDetails_AllRow_NormalMode
    [Arguments]    ${ac_ReportTableBody}    ${ac_Level_Login}
    [Documentation]    This action is used to calculate data of bettype in match below by inputing report body:
    ...
    ...    1.Total Winloss of the current user (VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM)
    ...    Winloss + Comm = Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    2.Total Winloss of Company (VerifyTotalWinlossCompany)
    ...    Downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company
    ...
    ...    ${ac_ReportTableBody}: the table body that contains row data need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Login}: Master/Agent/Member
    ## Set variable
    ${ac_boundary_number}    Set Variable    0.02
    ## Set Col Total for each level of report Winloss detail
    ${ac_MemberCol}    Set Variable    6
    ${ac_AgentCol}    Set Variable    9
    ${ac_MasterCol}    Set Variable    12
    ${ac_SuperCol}    Set Variable    15
    ${ac_CompanyCol} =    Set Variable if    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Super'    17    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Master'    14    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Agent'
    ...    11
    ## Get row and link downline
    ${ac_tr}    Set Variable    ${ac_ReportTableBody}
    ## Verify report Master Winloss Detail
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Super'    VerifyTotalMatchWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Login}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    ${ac_MemberCol}
    ...    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${ac_MasterCol}    ${ac_SuperCol}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Master'    VerifyTotalMatchWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Login}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    ${ac_MemberCol}
    ...    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${ac_MasterCol}    ${EMPTY}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Agent'    VerifyTotalMatchWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Login}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    ${ac_MemberCol}
    ...    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${EMPTY}    ${EMPTY}    ${ac_boundary_number}

VerifyTotalMatchWinlossDetails_AllRow_StackMode
    [Arguments]    ${ac_ReportTableBody}    ${ac_Level_Login}
    [Documentation]    This action is used to verify below by input report body:
    ...
    ...    1.Total Winloss of the current user (VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM)
    ...    Winloss + Comm = Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    2.Total Winloss of Company (VerifyTotalWinlossCompany)
    ...    Downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company
    ...
    ...    ${ac_RowID}: the row number that need to check. Start from 1. Input 0 means check all rows.
    ...
    ...    ${ac_ReportTableBody}: the table body that contains row data need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Report}: Master/Agent/Member
    ...
    ...    ${ac_isDrilldown}: drill down to downline member or not?
    ## Set variable
    ${ac_boundary_number}    Set Variable    0.02
    ## Get row and link downline
    ${ac_tr}    Set Variable    ${ac_ReportTableBody}
    ## Verify report Master Winloss Detail
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Super'    VerifyTotalMatchWinlossDetailsCompany_StackMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Login}    Yes    Yes
    ...    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Master'    VerifyTotalMatchWinlossDetailsCompany_StackMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Login}    No    Yes
    ...    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Login}'=='Agent'    VerifyTotalMatchWinlossDetailsCompany_StackMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Login}    No    No
    ...    ${ac_boundary_number}

VerifyTotalWinlossByRow_AllRow
    [Arguments]    ${ac_RowID}    ${ac_ReportTableBody}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_isDrilldown}=1
    [Documentation]    This action is used to verify below by input RowID:
    ...
    ...    1.Total Winloss of the current user (VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM)
    ...    Winloss + Comm = Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    2.Total Winloss of Company (VerifyTotalWinlossCompany)
    ...    Downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company
    ...
    ...    ${ac_RowID}: the row number that need to check. Start from 1. Input 0 means check all rows.
    ...
    ...    ${ac_ReportTableBody}: the table body that contains row data need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}: the column index of Super/Master/Agent/Member Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Report}: Master/Agent/Member
    ...
    ...    ${ac_isDrilldown}: drill down to downline member or not?
    ## Set variable
    ${ac_boundary_number}    Set Variable    0.02
    ${ac_StartRow}=    Set Variable If    '${ac_RowID}'=='0'    1    ${ac_RowID}
    ${ac_EndRow}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_RowID}'=='0'    Get Matching Xpath Count    ${ac_ReportTableBody}/tr
    ...    ELSE    Evaluate    ${ac_RowID}+1
    : FOR    ${ac_Row}    IN RANGE    ${ac_StartRow}    ${ac_EndRow}
    \    ## Set variable
    \    ${ac_tr}    Set Variable    ${ac_ReportTableBody}/tr[${ac_Row}]
    \    ${ac_lnkDownLine}    Set Variable    ${ac_tr}/td[1]/span
    \    ## Check Winloss of SMAM
    \    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    \    ## If report is MEMBER WIN LOSS, check Winloss of Member
    \    ${ac_col_Member_TotalWinloss}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS'    Evaluate    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss} - 3
    \    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS'    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_col_Member_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}
    \    ...    ${ac_boundary_number}
    \    ## Check Winloss of SMAM & Company
    \    Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossCompany_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Check Winloss of SMAM on Total Row
    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Check Winloss of Company on Total Row
    Smartphone VerifyTotalWinlossCompany_NormalMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Get report name after drilldown
    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}=    Set Variable if    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS'    AGENT WIN LOSS    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS'    MEMBER WIN LOSS    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS'
    ...    MEMBER BET LIST
    ## Drill down
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1'    Click Control    ${ac_lnkDownLine}
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1'    Verify Report General    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}    no

VerifyTotalWinlossByUsername
    [Arguments]    ${ac_DownlineName}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_isDrilldown}=1
    [Documentation]    This action is used to verify below by input DownlineName:
    ...    1. Total Winloss of the current user (VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM)
    ...    (eg, Winloss + Comm = Total Winloss)
    ...    2.Total Winloss of Company (VerifyTotalWinlossCompany)
    ...    Downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company
    ...
    ...    ${ac_DownlineName}: the username that need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}: the column index of Super/Master/Agent/Member Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Report}: Master/Agent/Member Win loss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_isDrilldown}: drill down to downline member or not?
    ## Set variable
    ${ac_lnkDownline}    Set Variable    //table[@id='tbl-rpt']/tbody//span[text()='${ac_DownlineName}']
    ${ac_tr}    Set Variable    ${ac_lnkDownline}/parent::td/parent::tr
    ${ac_boundary_number}    Set Variable    0.02
    ## Get report name after drilldown
    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}=    Set Variable if    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS'    AGENT WIN LOSS    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS'    MEMBER WIN LOSS    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS'
    ...    MEMBER BET LIST
    ## Check Winloss of SMAM
    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## If report is MEMBER WIN LOSS, check Winloss of Member
    ${ac_col_Member_TotalWinloss}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS'    Evaluate    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss} - 3
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS'    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_col_Member_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Check Winloss of Company
    VerifyTotalWinlossCompany    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Check Winloss of SMAM on Total Row
    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Check Winloss of Company on Total Row
    VerifyTotalWinlossCompany    ${in_m_report_TotalRow}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Drill down
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1'    Click Control    ${ac_lnkDownline}
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1'    Verify Report General    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}    no

VerifyTotalWinlossCompany
    [Arguments]    ${ac_Row}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}=0
    [Documentation]    Thisc action is used to verify Total Winloss of Company
    ...    Downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company*(-1)
    ...
    ...    ${ac_row}: the row that contains data need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}: the column index of Super/Master/Agent/Member Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Report}: Master/Agent/Member Winloss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_boundary_number}: the bound of error
    ## Set variable
    ${ac_col_DownlineTotal}    Evaluate    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss} - 3
    ${ac_col_Company}    Evaluate    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss} + 1
    ## get data on web
    ${ac_DownlineTotal_web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_DownlineTotal}]
    ${ac_Company_web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_Company}]
    ${ac_TotalWL_web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_TotalWinloss}]
    ## replace ',' if any
    ${ac_DownlineTotal_web}    Replace String    ${ac_DownlineTotal_web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_Company_web}    Replace String    ${ac_Company_web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_TotalWL_web}    Replace String    ${ac_TotalWL_web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ## Calculate Total WL based on WL and Com displayed on web
    ${ac_Company_Cal}    Evaluate    float(${ac_DownlineTotal_web})+ float(${ac_TotalWL_web})
    ## Get abs of sum(${ac_Company_web} + ${ac_Company_Cal})
    ${ac_Company_Cal_Web_abs}    Evaluate    abs(${ac_Company_web} + ${ac_Company_Cal})
    ## Verify data
    Should Be True    ${ac_Company_Cal_Web_abs}<=${ac_boundary_number}    ${ac_Level_Report} :Downline Total + Current Total Winloss <> Company

VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsByRow_AllRow
    [Arguments]    ${ac_RowID}    ${ac_ReportTableBody}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_isDrilldown}=1
    [Documentation]    This action is used to verify below by input report body:
    ...
    ...    1.Total Winloss of the current user (VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM)
    ...    Winloss + Comm = Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    2.Total Winloss of Company (VerifyTotalWinlossCompany)
    ...    Downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company
    ...
    ...    ${ac_RowID}: the row number that need to check. Start from 1. Input 0 means check all rows.

    ...    ${ac_ReportTableBody}: the table body that contains row data need to check

    ...    ${ac_Level_Report}: Master/Agent/Member
    ...
    ...    ${ac_isDrilldown}: drill down to downline member or not?

    ${ac_StartRow}=    Set Variable If    '${ac_RowID}'=='0'    1    ${ac_RowID}
    ${ac_EndRow}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_RowID}'=='0'    Get Matching Xpath Count    ${ac_ReportTableBody}/tr
    ...    ELSE    Evaluate    ${ac_RowID}+1
    ## Set Col Total for each level of report Winloss detail
    ${ac_MemberCol}    Set Variable    8
    ${ac_AgentCol}    Set Variable    11
    ${ac_MasterCol}    Set Variable    14
    ${ac_SuperCol}    Set Variable    17
    ${ac_CompanyCol} =    Set Variable if    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    18    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL'    15    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL'
    ...    12
    : FOR    ${ac_Row}    IN RANGE    ${ac_StartRow}    ${ac_EndRow}
    \    ## Get row and link downline
    \    ${ac_tr}    Set Variable    ${ac_ReportTableBody}/tr[${ac_Row}]
    \    ${ac_lnkDownline}    Set Variable    ${ac_tr}/td[1]/span
    \    ## Verify report Master Winloss Detail
    \    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}
    \    ...    ${ac_MemberCol}    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${ac_MasterCol}    ${ac_SuperCol}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    \    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail
    \    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}
    \    ...    ${ac_MemberCol}    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${ac_MasterCol}    ${EMPTY}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    \    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail
    \    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${ac_tr}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}
    \    ...    ${ac_MemberCol}    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${EMPTY}    ${EMPTY}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Master Winloss Detail on Total Row
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    ${ac_MemberCol}
    ...    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${ac_MasterCol}    ${ac_SuperCol}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail on Total Row
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    ${ac_MemberCol}
    ...    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${ac_MasterCol}    ${EMPTY}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Verify report Agent Winloss Detail on Total Row
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode    ${in_m_report_TotalRow}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_CompanyCol}    ${ac_MemberCol}
    ...    ${ac_AgentCol}    ${EMPTY}    ${EMPTY}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    ## Get report name after drilldown
    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}=    Set Variable if    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MASTER WIN LOSS DETAIL'    AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='AGENT WIN LOSS DETAIL'    MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL    '${ac_Level_Report}'=='MEMBER WIN LOSS DETAIL'
    ...    MEMBER BET LIST
    ## Drill down
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1'    Click Control    ${ac_lnkDownline}
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_isDrilldown}'=='1'    Verify Report General    ${ac_ReportNameAfterDrillDown}    no

VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_NormalMode
    [Arguments]    ${ac_Row}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_col_Company}    ${ac_col_MemTotal}    ${ac_col_AgentTotal}    ${ac_col_MasterTotal}=${EMPTY}
    ...    ${ac_col_SuperTotal}=${EMPTY}    ${ac_boundary_number}=0
    [Documentation]    Thisc action is used to verify Total Winloss of Company in report winloss details
    ...    All downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company
    ...    Eg:
    ...    - Master Winloss Detail report: Member total + Agent Total + Master Total + Super Total = Company*(-1)
    ...    - Agent Winloss Detail report: Member total + Agent Total + Master Total = Company*(-1)
    ...    - Member Winloss Detail report: Member total + Agent Total = Company*(-1)
    ...
    ...    ${ac_row}: the row that contains data need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Report}: Master/Agent/Member Win loss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_Company}: the column index of Company
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_MemTotal}: the column index of Member Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_AgentTotal}: the column index of Agent Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_MasterTotal}: the column index of Master Total Winloss. It could be empty.
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_SuperTotal}: the column index of Super Total Winloss. It could be empty.
    ...
    ...    ${ac_boundary_number}: the bound of error
    Comment    ## Set variable
    Comment    ${ac_boundary_number}    Set Variable    0.02
    ## get data on web
    ${ac_MemberTotal_Web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_MemTotal}]
    ${ac_AgentTotal_Web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_AgentTotal}]
    ${ac_MasterTotal_Web}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_col_MasterTotal}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_MasterTotal}]
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_SuperTotal_Web}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_col_SuperTotal}'!='${EMPTY}'    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_SuperTotal}]
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_Company_web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_Company}]
    ## replace ',' if any
    ${ac_MemberTotal_Web}    Replace String    ${ac_MemberTotal_Web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_AgentTotal_Web}    Replace String    ${ac_AgentTotal_Web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_MasterTotal_Web}    Replace String    ${ac_MasterTotal_Web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_SuperTotal_Web}    Replace String    ${ac_SuperTotal_Web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_Company_web}    Replace String    ${ac_Company_web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ## Calculate Total WL based on WL and Com displayed on web
    ${ac_Company_Cal}    Evaluate    ${ac_MemberTotal_Web} + ${ac_AgentTotal_Web} + ${ac_MasterTotal_Web} + ${ac_SuperTotal_Web}
    ## Get abs of sum(${ac_Company_web} + ${ac_Company_Cal})
    ${ac_Company_Cal_Web_abs}    Evaluate    abs(${ac_Company_web} + ${ac_Company_Cal})
    ## Verify data
    Should Be True    ${ac_Company_Cal_Web_abs}<=${ac_boundary_number}    ${ac_Level_Report} : All downline Total + Current Total Winloss <> Company
    ## Check Winloss of SMAM
    #Member
    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${ac_Row}    ${ac_col_MemTotal}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    #Agent
    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${ac_Row}    ${ac_col_AgentTotal}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    #Master
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_col_MasterTotal}'!='${EMPTY}'    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${ac_Row}    ${ac_col_MasterTotal}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}
    #Super
    Run Keyword If    '${ac_col_SuperTotal}'!='${EMPTY}'    VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode    ${ac_Row}    ${ac_col_SuperTotal}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}

VerifyTotalWinlossDetailsCompany_StackMode
    [Arguments]    ${ac_Row}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_col_Company}    ${ac_IsSuperColumn}=Yes    ${ac_IsMasterColumn}=Yes    ${ac_boundary_number}=0
    [Documentation]    Thisc action is used to verify Total Winloss of Company in report winloss details with *Stack mode*
    ...
    ...    All downline Total + Current Total Winloss = Company
    ...    Eg:
    ...    - Master Winloss Detail report: Member total + Agent Total + Master Total + Super Total = Company*(-1)
    ...    - Agent Winloss Detail report: Member total + Agent Total + Master Total = Company*(-1)
    ...    - Member Winloss Detail report: Member total + Agent Total = Company*(-1)
    ...
    ...    ${ac_row}: the row that contains data need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Report}: Master/Agent/Member Win loss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_Company}: the column index of Company
    ...
    ...    ${ac_IsSuperColumn}: Super columns existing or not? (Yes/No)
    ...
    ...    ${ac_IsMasterColumn}: Master columns existing or not? (Yes/No)
    ...
    ...    ${ac_boundary_number}: the bound of error
    Comment    ## Set variable
    Comment    ${ac_boundary_number}    Set Variable    0.02
    ## get data on web
    ${ac_MemberTotal_Web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[contains(@class,'col-member-winloss-combined')]//li[3]
    ${ac_AgentTotal_Web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[contains(@class,'col-agent-winloss-combined')]//li[3]
    ${ac_MasterTotal_Web}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_IsMasterColumn}'=='Yes'    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[contains(@class,'col-master-winloss-combined')]//li[3]
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_SuperTotal_Web}    Run Keyword If    '${ac_IsSuperColumn}'=='Yes'    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[contains(@class,'col-super-winloss-combined')]//li[3]
    ...    ELSE    Set Variable    0
    ${ac_Company_web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_Company}]
    ## replace ',' if any
    ${ac_MemberTotal_Web}    Replace String    ${ac_MemberTotal_Web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_AgentTotal_Web}    Replace String    ${ac_AgentTotal_Web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_MasterTotal_Web}    Replace String    ${ac_MasterTotal_Web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_SuperTotal_Web}    Replace String    ${ac_SuperTotal_Web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_Company_web}    Replace String    ${ac_Company_web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ## Calculate Total WL based on WL and Com displayed on web
    ${ac_Company_Cal}    Evaluate    ${ac_MemberTotal_Web} + ${ac_AgentTotal_Web} + ${ac_MasterTotal_Web} + ${ac_SuperTotal_Web}
    ## Get abs of sum(${ac_Company_web} + ${ac_Company_Cal})
    ${ac_Company_Cal_Web_abs}    Evaluate    abs(${ac_Company_web} + ${ac_Company_Cal})
    ## Verify data
    Should Be True    ${ac_Company_Cal_Web_abs}<=${ac_boundary_number}    ${ac_Level_Report} : All downline Total + Current Total Winloss <> Company
    ## Check Winloss of SMAM
    #Member

VerifyTotalWinlossSMAM_NormalMode
    [Arguments]    ${ac_Row}    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}    ${ac_Level_Report}    ${ac_boundary_number}=0
    [Documentation]    Thisc action is used to verify Total Winloss of Super/Master/Agent/Member
    ...
    ...    ${ac_row}: the row that contains data need to check
    ...
    ...    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss}: the column index of Super/Master/Agent/Member Total Winloss
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Level_Report}: Master/Agent/Member
    ...
    ...    ${ac_boundary_number}: the bound of error
    ## Set variable
    ${ac_col_WL}    Evaluate    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss} - 2
    ${ac_col_Comm}    Evaluate    ${ac_col_TotalWinloss} - 1
    ## get data on web
    ${ac_WL_web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_WL}]
    ${ac_Comm_web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_Comm}]
    ${ac_TotalWL_web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}/td[${ac_col_TotalWinloss}]
    ## replace ',' if any
    ${ac_WL_web}    Replace String    ${ac_WL_web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_Comm_web}    Replace String    ${ac_Comm_web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ${ac_TotalWL_web}    Replace String    ${ac_TotalWL_web}    ,    ${EMPTY}
    ## Calculate Total WL based on WL and Com displayed on web
    ${ac_TotalWL_Cal}    Evaluate    ${ac_WL_web} + ${ac_Comm_web}
    ## Get abs of (TotalWL_Web - TotalWL_Cal)
    ${ac_TotalWL_Cal_Web_abs}    Evaluate    abs(${ac_TotalWL_web} - ${ac_TotalWL_Cal})
    ## Verify data
    Should Be True    ${ac_TotalWL_Cal_Web_abs}<=${ac_boundary_number}    ${ac_Level_Report} : Winloss + Comm <> Total Winloss

    ${ac_TotalWL_web}    Get Text    ${ac_Row}//li[3]

    ${ac_TotalWL_web}    Replace String    ${ac_TotalWL_web}    ,    ${EMPTY}

Verify Product list in Report
    [Arguments]    ${ac_ReportName}
    [Documentation]    This action is used to verify Product list in Report. Compare product list with data in data set ProductList
    ...
    ...    ${ac_Reportname}: Report name
    ## Set variable
    ${ac_ProductControl}    Set Variable    //button[@data-toggle='dropdown']
    ${ac_ProductDdlControl}    Set Variable    //div[@class='btn-group open']/ul[@class='multiselect-container dropdown-menu']
    ## Get Product list from Data set Product list
    ${ac_lstExpectedProducts}    Set Variable If    '${ac_ReportName}'=='Match Win Loss Detail'    ${ds_strProductInReportMatchWL}    ${ds_strProductInReport}
    ${ac_lstExpectedProducts}    Split String    ${ac_lstExpectedProducts}    ,
    Append To List    ${ac_lstExpectedProducts}    Select all
    ${ac_lstReportWithLCS}    Set Variable    Win Loss Analysis, Win Loss Analysis Chart, Master Outstanding, Agent \ Outstanding, Member \ Outstanding
    ${ac_isLCS}    Evaluate    "${ac_lstReportWithLCS}".find("${ac_ReportName}")
    Run Keyword If    ${ac_isLCS}>=0    Append To List    ${ac_lstExpectedProducts}    Live Casino
    ## Open Product list to view list Product
    Wait Until Element Is Visible    ${ac_ProductControl}
    Click Element    ${ac_ProductControl}
    Wait Until Element Is Visible    ${ac_ProductDdlControl}
    ${ac_No}    Get Matching Xpath Count    ${ac_ProductDdlControl}/li
    ${ac_No}    Evaluate    ${ac_No} + 1
    ${ac_lstProduct}    Create List
    : FOR    ${ac_index}    IN RANGE    1    ${ac_No}
    \    ${ac_text}    Get Text    ${ac_ProductDdlControl}/li[${ac_index}]
    \    Append To List    ${ac_lstProduct}    ${ac_text}
    Compare Lists    ${ac_lstExpectedProducts}    ${ac_lstProduct}
